let loadedCount=0;
const totalRes=RESOURCES.length;
let gameStarted=false;
const loadBarFill=document.getElementById('loadBarFill');
const loadText=document.getElementById('loadText');
const loadDetail=document.getElementById('loadDetail');
const loadPct=document.getElementById('loadPct');
const loadingScreen=document.getElementById('loadingScreen');

function onResLoad(name){
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u2713 '+name;
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

function onResError(name){
  console.warn('Failed to load: '+name+' — using fallback');
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u26a0 '+name+' (fallback)';
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

// Start loading each resource
RESOURCES.forEach(function(r){
  r.img.onload=function(){onResLoad(r.name)};
  r.img.onerror=function(){onResError(r.name)};
  // Set src AFTER attaching handlers to ensure we catch the event
  r.img.src=r.src;
});

// Safety timeout: start game after 8 seconds even if some resources fail
setTimeout(function(){
  if(!gameStarted){
    console.warn('Resource load timeout — starting with fallbacks');
    startGame();
  }
},8000);

var __gameLoopStarted=false;
function startGame(){
  if(gameStarted)return;
  gameStarted=true;
  try{loadBarFill.style.width='100%';if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent='100%';loadText.textContent='Готово';loadDetail.textContent='Мир готов';}catch(_){}
  loadingScreen.style.transition='opacity 0.5s';
  loadingScreen.style.opacity='0';
  setTimeout(function(){
    loadingScreen.style.display='none';
    Promise.resolve(typeof ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap==='function'?ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap():false)
      .then(function(handled){
        if(handled)return;
        var fresh=ppaConsumeFreshStandaloneCharacter();
        if(fresh){
          INV.playerName=fresh.nickname;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=fresh.nickname;
          try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',fresh.nickname)}catch(_){}
          applyClass({name:fresh.className});beginGame();
          return;
        }
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      })
      .catch(function(){
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      });
  },500);
}

// === V219 GATEWAY / CLOUD SAVE BRIDGE ====================================
const PPA_CLOUD={
  ready:false,loading:false,version:null,timer:0,lastErrorAt:0,profile:null
};
function ppaCloudBridgeAvailable(){
  try{return !!(window.PPA&&typeof PPA.isAvailable==='function'&&PPA.isAvailable())}catch(_){return false}
}
function ppaCloudExtract(load){
  var root=load&&typeof load==='object'?load:{};
  var version=root.version;
  if(version==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')version=root.save.version;
  if(version==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')version=root.data.version;
  var state=root.state;
  if(state==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')state=root.save.state!=null?root.save.state:root.save.save;
  if(state==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')state=root.data.state!=null?root.data.state:root.data.save;
  if(state==null&&root.gameSave&&typeof root.gameSave==='object')state=root.gameSave.state||root.gameSave.save;
  if(typeof state==='string'){try{state=JSON.parse(state)}catch(_){state=null}}
  if(!state&&root&&typeof root==='object'&&(Array.isArray(root.bag)||root.equipped))state=root;
  return {version:version==null?null:Number(version),state:state&&typeof state==='object'?state:null};
}
function ppaCloudScheduleSave(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return;
  clearTimeout(PPA_CLOUD.timer);
  var snapshot=state&&typeof state==='object'?state:ppaBuildSaveObject();
  PPA_CLOUD.timer=setTimeout(function(){ppaCloudPush(snapshot)},2500);
}
async function ppaCloudPush(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable()||typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame!=='function')return false;
  try{
    var res=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(state||ppaBuildSaveObject(),PPA_CLOUD.version);
    if(res&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.version);
    else if(res&&res.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.save.version);
    return true;
  }catch(err){
    var now=Date.now();
    if(now-PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt>10000){
      PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt=now;
      console.warn('PPA cloud save:',err);
    }
    return false;
  }
}
function ppaProfileClassKey(profile){
  profile=profile&&typeof profile==='object'?profile:{};
  var raw=profile.classKey||profile.class_key||profile.characterClass||profile.character_class||profile.cls||profile.className||profile.class_name||'';
  raw=String(raw||'').trim();
  var aliases={
    'guardian':'tank','guard':'tank','страж':'tank',
    'paladin':'paladin','паладин':'paladin',
    'berserker':'barbarian','barbarian':'barbarian','берсерк':'barbarian',
    'mage':'mage','маг':'mage',
    'priest':'priest','жрец':'priest',
    'archer':'archer','лучник':'archer',
    'assassin':'assassin','ассасин':'assassin',
    'gnome':'gnome','gunner':'gnome','cannoneer':'gnome','гном-канонир':'gnome','гном канонир':'gnome'
  };
  var low=raw.toLowerCase();
  return aliases[low]||classKeyFromName(raw)||'';
}
function ppaProfileNickname(profile){
  profile=profile&&typeof profile==='object'?profile:{};
  return String(profile.nickname||profile.playerName||profile.player_name||profile.name||'').trim();
}
function ppaShowGatewayError(msg){
  try{
    var fr=document.getElementById('classSelectFrame');
    if(fr)fr.style.display='none';
    var el=document.getElementById('ppaGatewayError');
    if(!el){
      el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ppaGatewayError';
      el.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:10050;display:grid;place-items:center;padding:24px;background:#090708;color:#e7c98d;font:700 14px/1.5 Georgia,serif;text-align:center';
      document.body.appendChild(el);
    }
    el.innerHTML='<div style="max-width:520px;border:1px solid #6b4327;border-radius:12px;padding:18px;background:#160d0c">PHOENIX PIX ARENA<br><br><span style="color:#c9b9a1;font:11px monospace">'+String(msg||'Ошибка Telegram Gateway')+'</span></div>';
  }catch(_){}
}
function ppaMigrationSaveObject(){
  var out=ppaBuildSaveObject();
  var old=(P&&P._saved&&typeof P._saved==='object')?P._saved:null;
  if(old){
    ['lvl','xp','xn','kil','rebirths','statPts','talentPts','cls'].forEach(function(k){
      if(old[k]!=null)out[k]=old[k];
    });
    if(old.statAlloc&&typeof old.statAlloc==='object')out.statAlloc=Object.assign({},old.statAlloc);
    if(old.playerName&&!out.playerName)out.playerName=old.playerName;
  }
  return out;
}

let loadedCount=0;
const totalRes=RESOURCES.length;
let gameStarted=false;
const loadBarFill=document.getElementById('loadBarFill');
const loadText=document.getElementById('loadText');
const loadDetail=document.getElementById('loadDetail');
const loadPct=document.getElementById('loadPct');
const loadingScreen=document.getElementById('loadingScreen');

function onResLoad(name){
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u2713 '+name;
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

function onResError(name){
  console.warn('Failed to load: '+name+' — using fallback');
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u26a0 '+name+' (fallback)';
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

// Start loading each resource
RESOURCES.forEach(function(r){
  r.img.onload=function(){onResLoad(r.name)};
  r.img.onerror=function(){onResError(r.name)};
  // Set src AFTER attaching handlers to ensure we catch the event
  r.img.src=r.src;
});

// Safety timeout: start game after 8 seconds even if some resources fail
setTimeout(function(){
  if(!gameStarted){
    console.warn('Resource load timeout — starting with fallbacks');
    startGame();
  }
},8000);

var __gameLoopStarted=false;
function startGame(){
  if(gameStarted)return;
  gameStarted=true;
  try{loadBarFill.style.width='100%';if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent='100%';loadText.textContent='Готово';loadDetail.textContent='Мир готов';}catch(_){}
  loadingScreen.style.transition='opacity 0.5s';
  loadingScreen.style.opacity='0';
  setTimeout(function(){
    loadingScreen.style.display='none';
    Promise.resolve(typeof ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap==='function'?ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap():false)
      .then(function(handled){
        if(handled)return;
        var fresh=ppaConsumeFreshStandaloneCharacter();
        if(fresh){
          INV.playerName=fresh.nickname;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=fresh.nickname;
          try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',fresh.nickname)}catch(_){}
          applyClass({name:fresh.className});beginGame();
          return;
        }
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      })
      .catch(function(){
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      });
  },500);
}

// === V219 GATEWAY / CLOUD SAVE BRIDGE ====================================
const PPA_CLOUD={
  ready:false,loading:false,version:null,timer:0,lastErrorAt:0,profile:null
};
function ppaCloudBridgeAvailable(){
  try{return !!(window.PPA&&typeof PPA.isAvailable==='function'&&PPA.isAvailable())}catch(_){return false}
}
function ppaCloudExtract(load){
  var root=load&&typeof load==='object'?load:{};
  var version=root.version;
  if(version==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')version=root.save.version;
  if(version==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')version=root.data.version;
  var state=root.state;
  if(state==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')state=root.save.state!=null?root.save.state:root.save.save;
  if(state==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')state=root.data.state!=null?root.data.state:root.data.save;
  if(state==null&&root.gameSave&&typeof root.gameSave==='object')state=root.gameSave.state||root.gameSave.save;
  if(typeof state==='string'){try{state=JSON.parse(state)}catch(_){state=null}}
  if(!state&&root&&typeof root==='object'&&(Array.isArray(root.bag)||root.equipped))state=root;
  return {version:version==null?null:Number(version),state:state&&typeof state==='object'?state:null};
}
function ppaCloudScheduleSave(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return;
  clearTimeout(PPA_CLOUD.timer);
  var snapshot=state&&typeof state==='object'?state:ppaBuildSaveObject();
  PPA_CLOUD.timer=setTimeout(function(){ppaCloudPush(snapshot)},2500);
}
async function ppaCloudPush(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable()||typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame!=='function')return false;
  try{
    var res=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(state||ppaBuildSaveObject(),PPA_CLOUD.version);
    if(res&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.version);
    else if(res&&res.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.save.version);
    return true;
  }catch(err){
    var now=Date.now();
    if(now-PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt>10000){
      PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt=now;
      console.warn('PPA cloud save:',err);
    }
    return false;
  }
}
function ppaProfileClassKey(profile){
  profile=profile&&typeof profile==='object'?profile:{};
  var raw=profile.classKey||profile.class_key||profile.characterClass||profile.character_class||profile.cls||profile.className||profile.class_name||'';
  raw=String(raw||'').trim();
  var aliases={
    'guardian':'tank','guard':'tank','страж':'tank',
    'paladin':'paladin','паладин':'paladin',
    'berserker':'barbarian','barbarian':'barbarian','берсерк':'barbarian',
    'mage':'mage','маг':'mage',
    'priest':'priest','жрец':'priest',
    'archer':'archer','лучник':'archer',
    'assassin':'assassin','ассасин':'assassin',
    'gnome':'gnome','gunner':'gnome','cannoneer':'gnome','гном-канонир':'gnome','гном канонир':'gnome'
  };
  var low=raw.toLowerCase();
  return aliases[low]||classKeyFromName(raw)||'';
}
function ppaProfileNickname(profile){
  profile=profile&&typeof profile==='object'?profile:{};
  return String(profile.nickname||profile.playerName||profile.player_name||profile.name||'').trim();
}
function ppaShowGatewayError(msg){
  try{
    var fr=document.getElementById('classSelectFrame');
    if(fr)fr.style.display='none';
    var el=document.getElementById('ppaGatewayError');
    if(!el){
      el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ppaGatewayError';

      document.body.appendChild(el);
    }
    el.innerHTML='<div style="max-width:520px;border:1px solid #6b4327;border-radius:12px;padding:18px;background:#160d0c">PHOENIX PIX ARENA<br><br><span style="color:#c9b9a1;font:11px monospace">'+String(msg||'Ошибка Telegram Gateway')+'</span></div>';
  }catch(_){}
}
function ppaMigrationSaveObject(){
  var out=ppaBuildSaveObject();
  var old=(P&&P._saved&&typeof P._saved==='object')?P._saved:null;
  if(old){
    ['lvl','xp','xn','kil','rebirths','statPts','talentPts','cls'].forEach(function(k){
      if(old[k]!=null)out[k]=old[k];
    });
    if(old.statAlloc&&typeof old.statAlloc==='object')out.statAlloc=Object.assign({},old.statAlloc);
    if(old.playerName&&!out.playerName)out.playerName=old.playerName;
  }
  return out;
}

        cloudHasCharacter=true;

    }
    return false;

}

async function ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap(){
  if(!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return false;
  if(PPA_CLOUD.loading)return true;
  PPA_CLOUD.loading=true;
  try{
    if(typeof PPA.ppaAuthTelegram==='function')await PPA.ppaAuthTelegram();
    var profile=typeof PPA.ppaLoadProfile==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadProfile():null;
    profile=(profile&&profile.profile)||profile||{};
    PPA_CLOUD.profile=profile;

    var load=typeof PPA.ppaLoadSave==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadSave():null;
    var cloud=ppaCloudExtract(load);
    PPA_CLOUD.version=cloud.version;

    var forceMigration=localStorage.getItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219')==='1';
    var profileNick=ppaProfileNickname(profile);
    var savedNick=ppaLocalSavedNickname();
    var localCachePresent=ppaHasAnyLocalCharacterCache();
    var cloudHasCharacter=ppaSaveHasCharacterState(cloud.state);

    // Explicit portable-save import always wins: the player deliberately chose it.
    if(forceMigration&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
      var migrationState=ppaMigrationSaveObject();
      if(ppaSaveHasCharacterState(migrationState)){
        var migrated=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(migrationState,cloud.version);
        if(migrated&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.version);
        else if(migrated&&migrated.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.save.version);
        localStorage.removeItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219');
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:migrationState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }
    }

    // Existing cloud character is authoritative for this Telegram account.
    // Apply it directly to memory: no second resource-loading screen.
    if(cloudHasCharacter&&!forceMigration){
      var stamp='v'+String(cloud.version==null?'na':cloud.version);
      if(sessionStorage.getItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp')!==stamp){
        localStorage.setItem('pxSave',JSON.stringify(cloud.state));
        localStorage.setItem('pxSaveLastGood',JSON.stringify(cloud.state));
        if(cloud.state.playerName)try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',String(cloud.state.playerName))}catch(_){}
        sessionStorage.setItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp',stamp);
        if(!loadGame())console.warn('PPA: cloud save could not be applied in memory');
      }
    }

    // Empty cloud + old local cache is the dangerous case. Migrate only when
    // the local nickname belongs to the same profile. Otherwise this is a new
    // Telegram account on a device that still contains another player's cache.
    if(!cloudHasCharacter&&localCachePresent&&!forceMigration){
      var savedNickOk0=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(savedNick);
      var profileNickOk0=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(profileNick);
      if(savedNickOk0&&profileNickOk0&&ppaNickKey(savedNick)===ppaNickKey(profileNick)&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
        var initialState=ppaMigrationSaveObject();
        var first=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(initialState,cloud.version);
        if(first&&Number.isFinite(Number(first.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(first.version);
        else if(first&&first.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(first.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(first.save.version);
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:initialState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }else{
        console.info('PPA: new Telegram account detected; clearing foreign local character cache without reload.');
        ppaClearLocalCharacterCacheForAccountSwitch();
        ppaResetFreshCharacterInMemory(profileNick);
        savedNick=profileNick;
        localCachePresent=false;
      }
    }

    PPA_CLOUD.ready=true;

    // Re-read after possible migration/cleanup decision.
    savedNick=String(
      ((typeof INV!=='undefined'&&INV&&INV.playerName)||
       (P&&P._saved&&P._saved.playerName)||
       window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||
       localStorage.getItem('ppaPlayerNameV205')||'')
    ).trim();
    var savedNickOk=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(savedNick);
    var profileNickOk=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(profileNick);
    var nick=savedNickOk?savedNick:(profileNickOk?profileNick:'');
    if(nick){
      INV.playerName=nick;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=nick;
      try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',nick)}catch(_){}
    }
    // Existing V255 save wins during the one-time Gateway migration.
    // The server reads playerName from its own cloud save, so the client cannot
    // use this path as a free arbitrary rename.
    if(savedNickOk&&savedNick!==profileNick&&typeof PPA.ppaSyncNicknameFromSave==='function'){
      try{
        var syncNick=await PPA.ppaSyncNicknameFromSave();
        if(syncNick&&syncNick.ok&&syncNick.profile)PPA_CLOUD.profile=syncNick.profile;
        else if(syncNick&&syncNick.message)console.warn('PPA nickname migration:',syncNick.message);
      }catch(syncErr){console.warn('PPA nickname migration:',syncErr)}
    }

    var ck=(P._saved&&P._saved.cls)?classKeyFromName(P._saved.cls):'';
    if(!ck)ck=ppaProfileClassKey(profile);
    if(ck&&CLASS_BASE[ck]){
      applyClass({name:CLASS_BASE[ck].name});
      beginGame();
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }catch(err){
    console.error('PPA Gateway bootstrap:',err);
    ppaShowGatewayError('Не удалось подтвердить Telegr

// === V263 TEST CLEANUP / PICKAXE PPA ======================================
// Remove ONLY the temporary V260 test items that were tagged v260Test.
// Legitimate player gear/pets are not touched.
function v263RemoveV260TestItems(){
  let changed=false;

  if(INV.equipped&&typeof INV.equipped==='object'){
    Object.keys(INV.equipped).forEach(function(slot){
      const it=INV.equipped[slot];
      if(it&&it.v260Test===true){
        INV.equipped[slot]=null;
        changed=true;
      }
    });
  }

  if(Array.isArray(INV.bag)){
    const before=INV.bag.length;
    INV.bag=INV.bag.filter(function(it){return !(it&&it.v260Test===true)});
    if(INV.bag.length!==before)changed=true;
  }

  if(INV.storage&&typeof INV.storage==='object'){
    ['personal','clan','premium'].forEach(function(key){
      if(!Array.isArray(INV.storage[key]))return;
      const before=INV.storage[key].length;
      INV.storage[key]=INV.storage[key].filter(function(it){return !(it&&it.v260Test===true)});
      if(INV.storage[key].length!==before)changed=true;
    });
  }

  if(changed){
    recomputeStats();
    P.hp=Math.min(P.hp,P.mhp);
    P.mp=Math.min(P.mp,P.mmp);
    saveGame();
    try{
      sendInvState();
      sendBlacksmithState();
      sendStorageState();
      sendPremiumState();
    }catch(_){}
    updateUI();
    showPickup('Временный комплект удалён','#c9b28a');
  }
  return changed;
}

// Keep the earlier requested one-time PPA help for the 200-PPA pickaxe,
// but do not repeatedly top up PPA on every launch.
function v263EnsurePickaxePpa(){
  if(INV.fartPickaxe)return;
  if(INV.v263PickaxePpaGranted)return;
  INV.ppa=Math.max(200,Math.floor(Number(INV.ppa)||0));
  INV.v263PickaxePpaGranted=true;
  saveGame();
  try{sendInvState();sendBlacksmithState();updateUI()}catch(_){}
}

// Exact access rule requested: level 21 through level 40 inclusive.
function fartZoneLevelAllowed(){
  const lvl=Math.floor(Number(P.lvl)||1);
  return lvl>=21&&lvl<=40;
}

// === RELEASE CHARACTER LOAD ================================================
function applyClass(cls){
  if(!cls)return;

  const _classKey=classKeyFromName(cls.name)||'assassin';
  P.cls=CLASS_BASE[_classKey].name;
  P.anim='idle';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;P.attacking=false;
  P.dir8=4;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0;
  PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0;
  cancelSmartAttack();

  // Persistent character only: restore the saved level/progression FIRST,
  // then calculate the selected class base at that level.
  if(P._saved&&P._saved.lvl)P.lvl=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(P._saved.lvl)||1));
  applyClassBaseProfile(_classKey,P.lvl);

  if(P._saved){var sv=P._saved;
    if(sv.lvl)P.lvl=sv.lvl;
    if(sv.xp!=null)P.xp=sv.xp;
    if(sv.kil!=null)P.kil=sv.kil;
    P.xn=xpRequiredForLevel(P.lvl);
    if(typeof sv.statPts==='number')P.statPts=sv.statPts;
    else if(typeof sv.talentPts==='number')P.statPts=sv.talentPts;
    else P.statPts=10+Math.max(0,(P.lvl||1)-1)*3;
    P.statAlloc=Object.assign(
      {hp:0,mp:0,atk:0,def:0,spd:0,atkspd:0,critdmg:0,crit:0,dodge:0},
      sv.statAlloc||{}
    );
    P.rebirths=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(sv.rebirths)||0));
    repairStatPointProgress();
  }

  INV.books.forEach(function(b){
    if(b.applied){
      if(b.id==='fire')P.dmgMul=1.5;
      if(b.id==='ice')P.slowMul=0.7;
      if(b.id==='lightning')P.chain=2;
    }
  });

  P.sp=P.baseSp;
  recomputeStats();
  P.hp=P.mhp;
  P.mp=P.mmp;
  saveGame();
  updateUI();
}

function beginGame(){
  document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='none';
  v263RemoveV260TestItems();
  v263EnsurePickaxePpa();
  if(__gameLoopStarted)return;
  __gameLoopStarted=true;
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
  ln.innerHTML=sc.name+'<div class=\"sub\">'+sc.sub+'</div>';
  ln.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2500);
  PPAOnlineInit();
  let __v178LastLoopErrorAt=0;
  function loop(){
    try{
      update();
    }catch(err){
      const now=Date.now();
      if(now-__v178LastLoopErrorAt>1000){
        __v178LastLoopErrorAt=now;
        console.error('V178 update recovered',err);
      }
      // World-boss rewards must remain playable even if a secondary system
      // throws after the kill. The next frame continues normally.
      try{
        if(P.scene==='worldboss'&&WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active){
          transitioning=false;
          if(P.hp>0)P.dead=false;
          worldBossLootUpdate();
        }
      }catch(_){ }
    }
    try{
      render();
      try{if(window.__PPA_PERF_FRAME)window.__PPA_PERF_FRAME(performance.now())}catch(_){ }
    }catch(err){
      const now=Date.now();
      if(now-__v178LastLoopErrorAt>1000){
        __v178LastLoopErrorAt=now;
        console.error('V178 render recovered',err);
      }
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }
  loop();
}

let loadedCount=0;
const totalRes=RESOURCES.length;
let gameStarted=false;
const loadBarFill=document.getElementById('loadBarFill');
const loadText=document.getElementById('loadText');
const loadDetail=document.getElementById('loadDetail');
const loadPct=document.getElementById('loadPct');
const loadingScreen=document.getElementById('loadingScreen');

function onResLoad(name){
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u2713 '+name;
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

function onResError(name){
  console.warn('Failed to load: '+name+' — using fallback');
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u26a0 '+name+' (fallback)';
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

// Start loading each resource
RESOURCES.forEach(function(r){
  r.img.onload=function(){onResLoad(r.name)};
  r.img.onerror=function(){onResError(r.name)};
  // Set src AFTER attaching handlers to ensure we catch the event
  r.img.src=r.src;
});

// Safety timeout: start game after 8 seconds even if some resources fail
setTimeout(function(){
  if(!gameStarted){
    console.warn('Resource load timeout — starting with fallbacks');
    startGame();
  }
},8000);

var __gameLoopStarted=false;
function startGame(){
  if(gameStarted)return;
  gameStarted=true;
  try{loadBarFill.style.width='100%';if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent='100%';loadText.textContent='Готово';loadDetail.textContent='Мир готов';}catch(_){}
  loadingScreen.style.transition='opacity 0.5s';
  loadingScreen.style.opacity='0';
  setTimeout(function(){
    loadingScreen.style.display='none';
    Promise.resolve(typeof ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap==='function'?ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap():false)
      .then(function(handled){
        if(handled)return;
        var fresh=ppaConsumeFreshStandaloneCharacter();
        if(fresh){
          INV.playerName=fresh.nickname;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=fresh.nickname;
          try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',fresh.nickname)}catch(_){}
          applyClass({name:fresh.className});beginGame();
          return;
        }
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      })
      .catch(function(){
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      });
  },500);
}

// === V219 GATEWAY / CLOUD SAVE BRIDGE ====================================
const PPA_CLOUD={
  ready:false,loading:false,version:null,timer:0,lastErrorAt:0,profile:null
};
function ppaCloudBridgeAvailable(){
  try{return !!(window.PPA&&typeof PPA.isAvailable==='function'&&PPA.isAvailable())}catch(_){return false}
}
function ppaCloudExtract(load){
  var root=load&&typeof load==='object'?load:{};
  var version=root.version;
  if(version==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')version=root.save.version;
  if(version==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')version=root.data.version;
  var state=root.state;
  if(state==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')state=root.save.state!=null?root.save.state:root.save.save;
  if(state==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')state=root.data.state!=null?root.data.state:root.data.save;
  if(state==null&&root.gameSave&&typeof root.gameSave==='object')state=root.gameSave.state||root.gameSave.save;
  if(typeof state==='string'){try{state=JSON.parse(state)}catch(_){state=null}}
  if(!state&&root&&typeof root==='object'&&(Array.isArray(root.bag)||root.equipped))state=root;
  return {version:version==null?null:Number(version),state:state&&typeof state==='object'?state:null};
}
function ppaCloudScheduleSave(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return;
  clearTimeout(PPA_CLOUD.timer);
  var snapshot=state&&typeof state==='object'?state:ppaBuildSaveObject();
  PPA_CLOUD.timer=setTimeout(function(){ppaCloudPush(snapshot)},2500);
}
async function ppaCloudPush(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable()||typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame!=='function')return false;
  try{
    var res=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(state||ppaBuildSaveObject(),PPA_CLOUD.version);
    if(res&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.version);
    else if(res&&res.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.save.version);
    return true;
  }catch(err){
    var now=Date.now();
    if(now-PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt>10000){
      PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt=now;
      console.warn('PPA cloud save:',err);
    }
    return false;
  }
}
function ppaProfileClassKey(profile){
  profile=profile&&typeof profile==='object'?profile:{};
  var raw=profile.classKey||profile.class_key||profile.characterClass||profile.character_class||profile.cls||profile.className||profile.class_name||'';
  raw=String(raw||'').trim();
  var aliases={
    'guardian':'tank','guard':'tank','страж':'tank',
    'paladin':'paladin','паладин':'paladin',
    'berserker':'barbarian','barbarian':'barbarian','берсерк':'barbarian',
    'mage':'mage','маг':'mage',
    'priest':'priest','жрец':'priest',
    'archer':'archer','лучник':'archer',
    'assassin':'assassin','ассасин':'assassin',
    'gnome':'gnome','gunner':'gnome','cannoneer':'gnome','гном-канонир':'gnome','гном канонир':'gnome'
  };
  var low=raw.toLowerCase();
  return aliases[low]||classKeyFromName(raw)||'';
}
function ppaProfileNickname(profile){
  profile=profile&&typeof profile==='object'?profile:{};
  return String(profile.nickname||profile.playerName||profile.player_name||profile.name||'').trim();
}
function ppaShowGatewayError(msg){
  try{
    var fr=document.getElementById('classSelectFrame');
    if(fr)fr.style.display='none';
    var el=document.getElementById('ppaGatewayError');
    if(!el){
      el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ppaGatewayError';
      el.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:10050;display:grid;place-items:center;padding:24px;background:#090708;color:#e7c98d;font:700 14px/1.5 Georgia,serif;text-align:center';
      document.body.appendChild(el);
    }
    el.innerHTML='<div style="max-width:520px;border:1px solid #6b4327;border-radius:12px;padding:18px;background:#160d0c">PHOENIX PIX ARENA<br><br><span style="color:#c9b9a1;font:11px monospace">'+String(msg||'Ошибка Telegram Gateway')+'</span></div>';
  }catch(_){}
}
function ppaMigrationSaveObject(){
  var out=ppaBuildSaveObject();
  var old=(P&&P._saved&&typeof P._saved==='object')?P._saved:null;
  if(old){
    ['lvl','xp','xn','kil','rebirths','statPts','talentPts','cls'].forEach(function(k){
      if(old[k]!=null)out[k]=old[k];
    });
    if(old.statAlloc&&typeof old.statAlloc==='object')out.statAlloc=Object.assign({},old.statAlloc);
    if(old.playerName&&!out.playerName)out.playerName=old.playerName;
  }
  return out;
}

async function ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap(){
  if(!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return false;
  if(PPA_CLOUD.loading)return true;
  PPA_CLOUD.loading=true;
  try{
    if(typeof PPA.ppaAuthTelegram==='function')await PPA.ppaAuthTelegram();
    var profile=typeof PPA.ppaLoadProfile==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadProfile():null;
    profile=(profile&&profile.profile)||profile||{};
    PPA_CLOUD.profile=profile;

    var load=typeof PPA.ppaLoadSave==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadSave():null;
    var cloud=ppaCloudExtract(load);
    PPA_CLOUD.version=cloud.version;

    var forceMigration=localStorage.getItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219')==='1';
    var profileNick=ppaProfileNickname(profile);
    var savedNick=ppaLocalSavedNickname();
    var localCachePresent=ppaHasAnyLocalCharacterCache();
    var cloudHasCharacter=ppaSaveHasCharacterState(cloud.state);

    // Explicit portable-save import always wins: the player deliberately chose it.
    if(forceMigration&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
      var migrationState=ppaMigrationSaveObject();
      if(ppaSaveHasCharacterState(migrationState)){
        var migrated=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(migrationState,cloud.version);
        if(migrated&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.version);
        else if(migrated&&migrated.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.save.version);
        localStorage.removeItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219');
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:migrationState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }
    }

    // Existing cloud character is authoritative for this Telegram account.
    // Apply it directly to memory: no second resource-loading screen.
    if(cloudHasCharacter&&!forceMigration){
      var stamp='v'+String(cloud.version==null?'na':cloud.version);
      if(sessionStorage.getItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp')!==s

===== classSelect #3 @ 109710938 =====
an Boss 2 Cerberus Death',img:imgClanBoss2Death,src:ART_CLAN_BOSS_2_DEATH},
  ...SCENES.safe.npcs.map(function(n){return {name:'NPC '+n.label,img:n.img,src:n.imgSrc}}),
  ...(SCENES.fartzone.npcs||[]).map(function(n){return {name:'NPC '+n.label,img:n.img,src:n.imgSrc}}),
  ...DUNGEON_MOB_IMAGES.map(function(im,i){return {name:'Mob '+(i+1)+' '+MOB_TABLE[i].n,img:im,src:DUNGEON_MOB_SPRITES[i]}}),
  ...SLIME_SCAVENGER_IMAGES.map(function(im,i){return {name:'Slime Scavenger '+(i+1),img:im,src:SLIME_SCAVENGER_SPRITES[i]}}),
  {name:'Cave Spider 4x4 Atlas',img:CAVE_SPIDER_ATLAS,src:CAVE_SPIDER_ATLAS_SRC},
  ...Object.keys(MOB_ANIM_PACKS).map(function(k){
    const p=MOB_ANIM_PACKS[k];
    return {name:'Animated Mob '+k+' '+MOB_TABLE[Number(k)-1].n,img:p.img,src:p.src};
  }),
];

    el.innerHTML='<div style="max-width:520px;border:1px solid #6b4327;border-radius:12px;padding:18px;background:#160d0c">PHOENIX PIX ARENA<br><br><span style="color:#c9b9a1;font:11px monospace">'+String(msg||'Ошибка Telegram Gateway')+'</span></div>';
  }catch(_){}
}
function ppaMigrationSaveObject(){
  var out=ppaBuildSaveObject();
  var old=(P&&P._saved&&typeof P._saved==='object')?P._saved:null;
  if(old){
    ['lvl','xp','xn','kil','rebirths','statPts','talentPts','cls'].forEach(function(k){
      if(old[k]!=null)out[k]=old[k];
    });
    if(old.statAlloc&&typeof old.statAlloc==='object')out.statAlloc=Object.assign({},old.statAlloc);
    if(old.playerName&&!out.playerName)out.playerName=old.playerName;
  }
  return out;
}

async function ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap(){
  if(!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return false;
  if(PPA_CLOUD.loading)return true;
  PPA_CLOUD.loading=true;
  try{
    if(typeof PPA.ppaAuthTelegram==='function')await PPA.ppaAuthTelegram();
    var profile=typeof PPA.ppaLoadProfile==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadProfile():null;
    profile=(profile&&profile.profile)||profile||{};
    PPA_CLOUD.profile=profile;

    var load=typeof PPA.ppaLoadSave==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadSave():null;
    var cloud=ppaCloudExtract(load);
    PPA_CLOUD.version=cloud.version;

    var forceMigration=localStorage.getItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219')==='1';
    var profileNick=ppaProfileNickname(profile);
    var savedNick=ppaLocalSavedNickname();
    var localCachePresent=ppaHasAnyLocalCharacterCache();
    var cloudHasCharacter=ppaSaveHasCharacterState(cloud.state);

    // Explicit portable-save import always wins: the player deliberately chose it.
    if(forceMigration&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
      var migrationState=ppaMigrationSaveObject();
      if(ppaSaveHasCharacterState(migrationState)){
        var migrated=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(migrationState,cloud.version);
        if(migrated&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.version);
        else if(migrated&&migrated.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.save.version);
        localStorage.removeItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219');
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:migrationState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }
    }

    // Existing cloud character is authoritative for this Telegram account.
    // Apply it directly to memory: no second resource-loading screen.
    if(cloudHasCharacter&&!forceMigration){
      var stamp='v'+String(cloud.version==null?'na':cloud.version);
      if(sessionStorage.getItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp')!==stamp){
        localStorage.setItem('pxSave',JSON.stringify(cloud.state));
        localStorage.setItem('pxSaveLastGood',JSON.stringify(cloud.state));
        if(cloud.state.playerName)try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',String(cloud.state.playerName))}catch(_){}
        sessionStorage.setItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp',stamp);
        if(!loadGame())console.warn('PPA: cloud save could not be applied in memory');
      }
    }

    // Empty cloud + old local cache is the dangerous case. Migrate only when
    // the local nickname belongs to the same profile. Otherwise this is a new
    // Telegram account on a device that still contains another player's cache.
    if(!cloudHasCharacter&&localCachePresent&&!forceMigration){
      var savedNickOk0=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(savedNick);
      var profileNickOk0=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(profileNick);
      if(savedNickOk0&&profileNickOk0&&ppaNickKey(savedNick)===ppaNickKey(profileNick)&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
        var initialState=ppaMigrationSaveObject();
        var first=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(initialState,cloud.version);
        if(first&&Number.isFinite(Number(first.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(first.version);
        else if(first&&first.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(first.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(first.save.version);
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:initialState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }else{
        console.info('PPA: new Telegram account detected; clearing foreign local character cache without reload.');
        ppaClearLocalCharacterCacheForAccountSwitch();
        ppaResetFreshCharacterInMemory(profileNick);
        savedNick=profileNick;
        localCachePresent=false;
      }
    }

    PPA_CLOUD.ready=true;

    // Re-read after possible migration/cleanup decision.
    savedNick=String(
      ((typeof INV!=='undefined'&&INV&&INV.playerName)||
       (P&&P._saved&&P._saved.playerName)||
       window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||
       localStorage.getItem('ppaPlayerNameV205')||'')
    ).trim();
    var savedNickOk=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(savedNick);
    var profileNickOk=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(profileNick);
    var nick=savedNickOk?savedNick:(profileNickOk?profileNick:'');
    if(nick){
      INV.playerName=nick;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=nick;
      try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',nick)}catch(_){}
    }
    // Existing V255 save wins during the one-time Gateway migration.
    // The server reads playerName from its own cloud save, so the client cannot
    // use this path as a free arbitrary rename.
    if(savedNickOk&&savedNick!==profileNick&&typeof PPA.ppaSyncNicknameFromSave==='function'){
      try{
        var syncNick=await PPA.ppaSyncNicknameFromSave();
        if(syncNick&&syncNick.ok&&syncNick.profile)PPA_CLOUD.profile=syncNick.profile;
        else if(syncNick&&syncNick.message)console.warn('PPA nickname migration:',syncNick.message);
      }catch(syncErr){console.warn('PPA nickname migration:',syncErr)}
    }

    var ck=(P._saved&&P._saved.cls)?classKeyFromName(P._saved.cls):'';
    if(!ck)ck=ppaProfileClassKey(profile);
    if(ck&&CLASS_BASE[ck]){
      applyClass({name:CLASS_BASE[ck].name});
      beginGame();
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }catch(err){
    console.error('PPA Gateway bootstrap:',err);
    ppaShowGatewayError('Не удалось подтвердить Telegr

===== classSelect #5 @ 109724381 =====


// === V263 TEST CLEANUP / PICKAXE PPA ======================================
// Remove ONLY the temporary V260 test items that were tagged v260Test.
// Legitimate player gear/pets are not touched.
function v263RemoveV260TestItems(){
  let changed=false;

  if(INV.equipped&&typeof INV.equipped==='object'){
    Object.keys(INV.equipped).forEach(function(slot){
      const it=INV.equipped[slot];
      if(it&&it.v260Test===true){
        INV.equipped[slot]=null;
        changed=true;
      }
    });
  }

  if(Array.isArray(INV.bag)){
    const before=INV.bag.length;
    INV.bag=INV.bag.filter(function(it){return !(it&&it.v260Test===true)});
    if(INV.bag.length!==before)changed=true;
  }

  if(INV.storage&&typeof INV.storage==='object'){
    ['personal','clan','premium'].forEach(function(key){
      if(!Array.isArray(INV.storage[key]))return;
      const before=INV.storage[key].length;
      INV.storage[key]=INV.storage[key].filter(function(it){return !(it&&it.v260Test===true)});
      if(INV.storage[key].length!==before)changed=true;
    });
  }

  if(changed){
    recomputeStats();
    P.hp=Math.min(P.hp,P.mhp);
    P.mp=Math.min(P.mp,P.mmp);
    saveGame();
    try{
      sendInvState();
      sendBlacksmithState();
      sendStorageState();
      sendPremiumState();
    }catch(_){}
    updateUI();
    showPickup('Временный комплект удалён','#c9b28a');
  }
  return changed;
}

// Keep the earlier requested one-time PPA help for the 200-PPA pickaxe,
// but do not repeatedly top up PPA on every launch.
function v263EnsurePickaxePpa(){
  if(INV.fartPickaxe)return;
  if(INV.v263PickaxePpaGranted)return;
  INV.ppa=Math.max(200,Math.floor(Number(INV.ppa)||0));
  INV.v263PickaxePpaGranted=true;
  saveGame();
  try{sendInvState();sendBlacksmithState();updateUI()}catch(_){}
}

// Exact access rule requested: level 21 through level 40 inclusive.
function fartZoneLevelAllowed(){
  const lvl=Math.floor(Number(P.lvl)||1);
  return lvl>=21&&lvl<=40;
}

// === RELEASE CHARACTER LOAD ================================================
function applyClass(cls){
  if(!cls)return;

  const _classKey=classKeyFromName(cls.name)||'assassin';
  P.cls=CLASS_BASE[_classKey].name;
  P.anim='idle';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;P.attacking=false;
  P.dir8=4;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0;
  PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0;
  cancelSmartAttack();

  // Persistent character only: restore the saved level/progression FIRST,
  // then calculate the selected class base at that level.
  if(P._saved&&P._saved.lvl)P.lvl=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(P._saved.lvl)||1));
  applyClassBaseProfile(_classKey,P.lvl);

  if(P._saved){var sv=P._saved;
    if(sv.lvl)P.lvl=sv.lvl;
    if(sv.xp!=null)P.xp=sv.xp;
    if(sv.kil!=null)P.kil=sv.kil;
    P.xn=xpRequiredForLevel(P.lvl);
    if(typeof sv.statPts==='number')P.statPts=sv.statPts;
    else if(typeof sv.talentPts==='number')P.statPts=sv.talentPts;
    else P.statPts=10+Math.max(0,(P.lvl||1)-1)*3;
    P.statAlloc=Object.assign(
      {hp:0,mp:0,atk:0,def:0,spd:0,atkspd:0,critdmg:0,crit:0,dodge:0},
      sv.statAlloc||{}
    );
    P.rebirths=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(sv.rebirths)||0));
    repairStatPointProgress();
  }

  INV.books.forEach(function(b){
    if(b.applied){
      if(b.id==='fire')P.dmgMul=1.5;
      if(b.id==='ice')P.slowMul=0.7;
      if(b.id==='lightning')P.chain=2;
    }
  });

  P.sp=P.baseSp;
  recomputeStats();
  P.hp=P.mhp;
  P.mp=P.mmp;
  saveGame();
  updateUI();
}

function beginGame(){
  document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='none';
  v263RemoveV260TestItems();
  v263EnsurePickaxePpa();
  if(__gameLoopStarted)return;
  __gameLoopStarted=true;
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
  ln.innerHTML=sc.name+'<div class=\"sub\">'+sc.sub+'</div>';
  ln.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2500);
  PPAOnlineInit();
  let __v178LastLoopErrorAt=0;
  function loop(){
    try{
      update();
    }catch(err){
      const now=Date.now();
      if(now-__v178LastLoopErrorAt>1000){
        __v178LastLoopErrorAt=now;
        console.error('V178 update recovered',err);
      }
      // World-boss rewards must remain playable even if a secondary system
      // throws after the kill. The next frame continues normally.
      try{
        if(P.scene==='worldboss'&&WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active){
          transitioning=false;
          if(P.hp>0)P.dead=false;
          worldBossLootUpdate();
        }
      }catch(_){ }
    }
    try{
      render();
      try{if(window.__PPA_PERF_FRAME)window.__PPA_PERF_FRAME(performance.now())}catch(_){ }
    }catch(err){
      const now=Date.now();
      if(now-__v178LastLoopErrorAt>1000){
        __v178LastLoopErrorAt=now;
        console.error('V178 render recovered',err);
      }
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }
  loop();
}

let loadedCount=0;
const totalRes=RESOURCES.length;
let gameStarted=false;
const loadBarFill=document.getElementById('loadBarFill');
const loadText=document.getElementById('loadText');
const loadDetail=document.getElementById('loadDetail');
const loadPct=document.getElementById('loadPct');
const loadingScreen=document.getElementById('loadingScreen');

function onResLoad(name){
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u2713 '+name;
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

function onResError(name){
  console.warn('Failed to load: '+name+' — using fallback');
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u26a0 '+name+' (fallback)';
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

// Start loading each resource
RESOURCES.forEach(function(r){
  r.img.onload=function(){onResLoad(r.name)};
  r.img.onerror=function(){onResError(r.name)};
  // Set src AFTER attaching handlers to ensure we catch the event
  r.img.src=r.src;
});

// Safety timeout: start game after 8 seconds even if some resources fail
setTimeout(function(){
  if(!gameStarted){
    console.warn('Resource load timeout — starting with fallbacks');
    startGame();
  }
},8000);

var __gameLoopStarted=false;
function startGame(){
  if(gameStarted)return;
  gameStarted=true;
  try{loadBarFill.style.width='100%';if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent='100%';loadText.textContent='Готово';loadDetail.textContent='Мир готов';}catch(_){}
  loadingScreen.style.transition='opacity 0.5s';
  loadingScreen.style.opacity='0';
  setTimeout(function(){
    loadingScreen.style.display='none';
    Promise.resolve(typeof ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap==='function'?ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap():false)
      .then(function(handled){
        if(handled)return;
        var fresh=ppaConsumeFreshStandaloneCharacter();
        if(fresh){
          INV.playerName=fresh.nickname;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=fresh.nickname;
          try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',fresh.nickname)}catch(_){}
          applyClass({name:fresh.className});beginGame();
          return;
        }
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      })
      .catch(function(){
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      });
  },500);
}

// === V219 GATEWAY / CLOUD SAVE BRIDGE ====================================
const PPA_CLOUD={
  ready:false,loading:false,version:null,timer:0,lastErrorAt:0,profile:null
};
function ppaCloudBridgeAvailable(){
  try{return !!(window.PPA&&typeof PPA.isAvailable==='function'&&PPA.isAvailable())}catch(_){return false}
}
function ppaCloudExtract(load){
  var root=load&&typeof load==='object'?load:{};
  var version=root.version;
  if(version==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')version=root.save.version;
  if(version==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')version=root.data.version;
  var state=root.state;
  if(state==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')state=root.save.state!=null?root.save.state:root.save.save;
  if(state==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')state=root.data.state!=null?root.data.state:root.data.save;
  if(state==null&&root.gameSave&&typeof root.gameSave==='object')state=root.gameSave.state||root.gameSave.save;
  if(typeof state==='string'){try{state=JSON.parse(state)}catch(_){state=null}}
  if(!state&&root&&typeof root==='object'&&(Array.isArray(root.bag)||root.equipped))state=root;
  return {version:version==null?null:Number(version),state:state&&typeof state==='object'?state:null};
}
function ppaCloudScheduleSave(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return;
  clearTimeout(PPA_CLOUD.timer);
  var snapshot=state&&typeof state==='object'?state:ppaBuildSaveObject();
  PPA_CLOUD.timer=setTimeout(function(){ppaCloudPush(snapshot)},2500);
}
async function ppaCloudPush(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable()||typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame!=='function')return false;
  try{
    var res=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(state||ppaBuildSaveObject(),PPA_CLOUD.version);
    if(res&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.version);
    else if(res&&res.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.save.version);
    return true;
  }catch(err){
    var now=Date.now();
    if(now-PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt>10000){
      PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt=now;
      console.warn('PPA cloud save:',err);
    }
    return false;
  }
}
function ppaProfileClassKey(profile){
  profile=profile&&typeof profile==='object'?profile:{};
  var raw=profile.classKey||profile.class_key||profile.characterClass||profile.character_class||profile.cls||profile.className||profile.class_name||'';
  raw=String(raw||'').trim();
  var aliases={
    'guardian':'tank','guard':'tank','страж':'tank',
    'paladin':'paladin','паладин':'paladin',
    'berserker':'barbarian','barbarian':'barbarian','берсерк':'barbarian',
    'mage':'mage','маг':'mage',
    'priest':'priest','жрец':'priest',
    'archer':'archer','лучник':'archer',
    'assassin':'assassin','ассасин':'assassin',
    'gnome':'gnome','gunner':'gnome','cannoneer':'gnome','гном-канонир':'gnome','гном канонир':'gnome'
  };
  var low=raw.toLowerCase();
  return aliases[low]||classKeyFromName(raw)||'';
}
function ppaProfileNickname(profile){
  profile=profile&&typeof profile==='object'?profile:{};
  return String(profile.nickname||profile.playerName||profile.player_name||profile.name||'').trim();
}
function ppaShowGatewayError(msg){
  try{
    var fr=document.getElementById('classSelectFrame');
    if(fr)fr.style.display='none';
    var el=document.getElementById('ppaGatewayError');
    if(!el){
      el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ppaGatewayError';
      el.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:10050;display:grid;place-items:center;padding:24px;background:#090708;color:#e7c98d;font:700 14px/1.5 Georgia,serif;text-align:center';
      document.body.appendChild(el);
    }
    el.innerHTML='<div style="max-width:520px;border:1px solid #6b4327;border-radius:12px;padding:18px;background:#160d0c">PHOENIX PIX ARENA<br><br><span style="color:#c9b9a1;font:11px monospace">'+String(msg||'Ошибка Telegram Gateway')+'</span></div>';
  }catch(_){}
}
function ppaMigrationSaveObject(){
  var out=ppaBuildSaveObject();
  var old=(P&&P._saved&&typeof P._saved==='object')?P._saved:null;
  if(old){
    ['lvl','xp','xn','kil','rebirths','statPts','talentPts','cls'].forEach(function(k){
      if(old[k]!=null)out[k]=old[k];
    });
    if(old.statAlloc&&typeof old.statAlloc==='object')out.statAlloc=Object.assign({},old.statAlloc);
    if(old.playerName&&!out.playerName)out.playerName=old.playerName;
  }
  return out;
}

async function ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap(){
  if(!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return false;
  if(PPA_CLOUD.loading)return true;
  PPA_CLOUD.loading=true;
  try{
    if(typeof PPA.ppaAuthTelegram==='function')await PPA.ppaAuthTelegram();
    var profile=typeof PPA.ppaLoadProfile==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadProfile():null;
    profile=(profile&&profile.profile)||profile||{};
    PPA_CLOUD.profile=profile;

    var load=typeof PPA.ppaLoadSave==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadSave():null;
    var cloud=ppaCloudExtract(load);
    PPA_CLOUD.version=cloud.version;

    var forceMigration=localStorage.getItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219')==='1';
    var profileNick=ppaProfileNickname(profile);
    var savedNick=ppaLocalSavedNickname();
    var localCachePresent=ppaHasAnyLocalCharacterCache();
    var cloudHasCharacter=ppaSaveHasCharacterState(cloud.state);

    // Explicit portable-save import always wins: the player deliberately chose it.
    if(forceMigration&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
      var migrationState=ppaMigrationSaveObject();
      if(ppaSaveHasCharacterState(migrationState)){
        var migrated=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(migrationState,cloud.version);
        if(migrated&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.version);
        else if(migrated&&migrated.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.save.version);
        localStorage.removeItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219');
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:migrationState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }
    }

    // Existing cloud character is authoritative for this Telegram account.
    // Apply it directly to memory: no second resource-loading screen.
    if(cloudHasCharacter&&!forceMigration){
      var stamp='v'+String(cloud.version==null?'na':

===== beginGame() #2 @ 109720577 =====
m account.
    // Apply it directly to memory: no second resource-loading screen.
    if(cloudHasCharacter&&!forceMigration){
      var stamp='v'+String(cloud.version==null?'na':cloud.version);
      if(sessionStorage.getItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp')!==stamp){
        localStorage.setItem('pxSave',JSON.stringify(cloud.state));
        localStorage.setItem('pxSaveLastGood',JSON.stringify(cloud.state));
        if(cloud.state.playerName)try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',String(cloud.state.playerName))}catch(_){}
        sessionStorage.setItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp',stamp);
        if(!loadGame())console.warn('PPA: cloud save could not be applied in memory');
      }
    }

    // Empty cloud + old local cache is the dangerous case. Migrate only when
    // the local nickname belongs to the same profile. Otherwise this is a new
    // Telegram account on a device that still contains another player's cache.
    if(!cloudHasCharacter&&localCachePresent&&!forceMigration){
      var savedNickOk0=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(savedNick);
      var profileNickOk0=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(profileNick);
      if(savedNickOk0&&profileNickOk0&&ppaNickKey(savedNick)===ppaNickKey(profileNick)&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
        var initialState=ppaMigrationSaveObject();
        var first=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(initialState,cloud.version);
        if(first&&Number.isFinite(Number(first.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(first.version);
        else if(first&&first.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(first.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(first.save.version);
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:initialState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }else{
        console.info('PPA: new Telegram account detected; clearing foreign local character cache without reload.');
        ppaClearLocalCharacterCacheForAccountSwitch();
        ppaResetFreshCharacterInMemory(profileNick);
        savedNick=profileNick;
        localCachePresent=false;
      }
    }

    PPA_CLOUD.ready=true;

    // Re-read after possible migration/cleanup decision.
    savedNick=String(
      ((typeof INV!=='undefined'&&INV&&INV.playerName)||
       (P&&P._saved&&P._saved.playerName)||
       window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||
       localStorage.getItem('ppaPlayerNameV205')||'')
    ).trim();
    var savedNickOk=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(savedNick);
    var profileNickOk=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(profileNick);
    var nick=savedNickOk?savedNick:(profileNickOk?profileNick:'');
    if(nick){
      INV.playerName=nick;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=nick;
      try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',nick)}catch(_){}
    }
    // Existing V255 save wins during the one-time Gateway migration.
    // The server reads playerName from its own cloud save, so the client cannot
    // use this path as a free arbitrary rename.
    if(savedNickOk&&savedNick!==profileNick&&typeof PPA.ppaSyncNicknameFromSave==='function'){
      try{
        var syncNick=await PPA.ppaSyncNicknameFromSave();
        if(syncNick&&syncNick.ok&&syncNick.profile)PPA_CLOUD.profile=syncNick.profile;
        else if(syncNick&&syncNick.message)console.warn('PPA nickname migration:',syncNick.message);
      }catch(syncErr){console.warn('PPA nickname migration:',syncErr)}
    }

    var ck=(P._saved&&P._saved.cls)?classKeyFromName(P._saved.cls):'';
    if(!ck)ck=ppaProfileClassKey(profile);
    if(ck&&CLASS_BASE[ck]){
      applyClass({name:CLASS_BASE[ck].name});
      beginGame();
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }catch(err){
    console.error('PPA Gateway bootstrap:',err);
    ppaShowGatewayError('Не удалось подтвердить Telegram-сессию. Закройте Mini App и откройте игру снова через бота.');
    return true;
  }finally{
    PPA_CLOUD.loading=false;
  }
}


// === V263 TEST CLEANUP / PICKAXE PPA ======================================
// Remove ONLY the temporary V260 test items that were tagged v260Test.
// Legitimate player gear/pets are not touched.
function v263RemoveV260TestItems(){
  let changed=false;

  if(INV.equipped&&typeof INV.equipped==='object'){
    Object.keys(INV.equipped).forEach(function(slot){
      const it=INV.equipped[slot];
      if(it&&it.v260Test===true){
        INV.equipped[slot]=null;
        changed=true;
      }
    });
  }

  if(Array.isArray(INV.bag)){
    const before=INV.bag.length;
    INV.bag=INV.bag.filter(function(it){return !(it&&it.v260Test===true)});
    if(INV.bag.length!==before)changed=true;
  }

  if(INV.storage&&typeof INV.storage==='object'){
    ['personal','clan','premium'].forEach(function(key){
      if(!Array.isArray(INV.storage[key]))return;
      const before=INV.storage[key].length;
      INV.storage[key]=INV.storage[key].filter(function(it){return !(it&&it.v260Test===true)});
      if(INV.storage[key].length!==before)changed=true;
    });
  }

  if(changed){
    recomputeStats();
    P.hp=Math.min(P.hp,P.mhp);
    P.mp=Math.min(P.mp,P.mmp);
    saveGame();
    try{
      sendInvState();
      sendBlacksmithState();
      sendStorageState();
      sendPremiumState();
    }catch(_){}
    updateUI();
    showPickup('Временный комплект удалён','#c9b28a');
  }
  return changed;
}

// Keep the earlier requested one-time PPA help for the 200-PPA pickaxe,
// but do not repeatedly top up PPA on every launch.
function v263EnsurePickaxePpa(){
  if(INV.fartPickaxe)return;
  if(INV.v263PickaxePpaGranted)return;
  INV.ppa=Math.max(200,Math.floor(Number(INV.ppa)||0));
  INV.v263PickaxePpaGranted=true;
  saveGame();
  try{sendInvState();sendBlacksmithState();updateUI()}catch(_){}
}

// Exact access rule requested: level 21 through level 40 inclusive.
function fartZoneLevelAllowed(){
  const lvl=Math.floor(Number(P.lvl)||1);
  return lvl>=21&&lvl<=40;
}

// === RELEASE CHARACTER LOAD ================================================
function applyClass(cls){
  if(!cls)return;

  const _classKey=classKeyFromName(cls.name)||'assassin';
  P.cls=CLASS_BASE[_classKey].name;
  P.anim='idle';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;P.attacking=false;
  P.dir8=4;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0;
  PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0;
  cancelSmartAttack();

  // Persistent character only: restore the saved level/progression FIRST,
  // then calculate the selected class base at that level.
  if(P._saved&&P._saved.lvl)P.lvl=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(P._saved.lvl)||1));
  applyClassBaseProfile(_classKey,P.lvl);

  if(P._saved){var sv=P._saved;
    if(sv.lvl)P.lvl=sv.lvl;
    if(sv.xp!=null)P.xp=sv.xp;
    if(sv.kil!=null)P.kil=sv.kil;
    P.xn=xpRequiredForLevel(P.lvl);
    if(typeof sv.statPts==='number')P.statPts=sv.statPts;
    else if(typeof sv.talentPts==='number')P.statPts=sv.talentPts;
    else P.statPts=10+Math.max(0,(P.lvl||1)-1)*3;
    P.statAlloc=Object.assign(
      {hp:0,mp:0,atk:0,def:0,spd:0,atkspd:0,critdmg:0,crit:0,dodge:0},
      sv.statAlloc||{}
    );
    P.rebirths=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(sv.rebirths)||0));
    repairStatPointProgress();
  }

  INV.books.forEach(function(b){
    if(b.applied){
      if(b.id==='fire')P.dmgMul=1.5;
      if(b.id==='ice')P.slowMul=0.7;
      if(b.id==='lightning')P.chain=2;
    }
  });

  P.sp=P.baseSp;
  recomputeStats();
  P.hp=P.mhp;
  P.mp=P.mmp;
  saveGame();
  updateUI();
}

function beginGame(){
  document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='none';
  v263RemoveV260TestItems();
  v263EnsurePickaxePpa();
  if(__gameLoopStarted)return;
  __gameLoopStarted=true;
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
  ln.innerHTML=sc.name+'<div class=\"sub\">'+sc.sub+'</div>';
  ln.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2500);
  PPAOnlineInit();
  let __v178LastLoopErrorAt=0;
  function loop(){
    try{
      update();
    }catch(err){
      const now=Date.now();
      if(now-__v178LastLoopErrorAt>1000){
        __v178LastLoopErrorAt=now;
        console.error('V178 update recovered',err);
      }
      // World-boss rewards must remain playable even if a secondary system
      // throws after the kill. The next frame continues normally.
      try{
        if(P.scene==='worldboss'&&WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active){
          transitioning=false;
          if(P.hp>0)P.dead=false;
          worldBossLootUpdate();
        }
      }catch(_){ }
    }
    try{
      render();
      try{if(window.__PPA_PERF_FRAME)window.__PPA_PERF_FRAME(performance.now())}catch(_){ }
    }catch(err){
      const now=Date.now();
      if(now-__v178LastLoopErrorAt>1000){
        __v178LastLoopErrorAt=now;
        console.error('V178 render recovered',err);
      }
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }
  loop();
}

function gramWalletTelegramUser(){
  try{
    var u=window.Telegram&&Telegram.WebApp&&Telegram.WebApp.initDataUnsafe&&Telegram.WebApp.initDataUnsafe.user;
    if(u&&u.id){
      var n=[u.first_name,u.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim()||u.username||('ID '+u.id);
      return {id:String(u.id),name:n,username:u.username||''};
    }
  }catch(_){}
  return {id:'',name:'Вне Telegram',username:''};
}
function gramWalletRoot(){
  if(!INV.gramWallet||typeof INV.gramWallet!=='object')INV.gramWallet={profiles:{}};
  if(!INV.gramWallet.profiles||typeof INV.gramWallet.profiles!=='object')INV.gramWallet.profiles={};
  return INV.gramWallet;
}
function gramWalletProfile(){
  var root=gramWalletRoot(),tg=gramWalletTelegramUser(),key=String(tg.id||'offline');
  var p=root.profiles[key];
  if(!p||typeof p!=='object')p=root.profiles[key]={connected:false,address:'',history:[],walletGram:null,updatedAt:0};
  p.connected=!!p.connected;p.address=String(p.address||'');if(!Array.isArray(p.history))p.history=[];
  if(!Number.isFinite(Number(p.walletGram)))p.walletGram=null;else p.walletGram=Math.max(0,Number(p.walletGram));
  return p;
}
function gramWalletHistory(text,amountText){
  var p=gramWalletProfile();p.history.push({time:Date.now(),text:String(text||'Операция'),amountText:String(amountText||'')});
  if(p.history.length>100)p.history=p.history.slice(-100);p.updatedAt=Date.now();
}
function gramWalletValidTonAddress(v){
  v=String(v||'').trim();
  return /^(?:EQ|UQ|kQ|0Q)[A-Za-z0-9_-]{46}$/.test(v)||/^(?:-1|0):[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/.test(v);
}
function gramWalletResult(ok,msg){
  try{if(gramWalletFrame&&gramWalletFrame.contentWindow)gramWalletFrame.contentWindow.postMessage({type:'gramWalletResult',ok:!!ok,message:String(msg||'')},'*')}catch(_){}
}
function sendGramWalletState(){
  try{
    var p=gramWalletProfile(),tg=gramWalletTelegramUser();
    if(gramWalletFrame&&gramWalletFrame.contentWindow)gramWalletFrame.contentWindow.postMessage({
      type:'gramWalletState',connected:!!p.connected,address:p.address||'',gameGram:Math.max(0,Number(INV.gram)||0),
      walletGram:p.walletGram,history:p.history||[],telegram:tg,withdrawFeePct:10
    },'*');
  }catch(_){}
}

let loadedCount=0;
const totalRes=RESOURCES.length;
let gameStarted=false;
const loadBarFill=document.getElementById('loadBarFill');
const loadText=document.getElementById('loadText');
const loadDetail=document.getElementById('loadDetail');
const loadPct=document.getElementById('loadPct');
const loadingScreen=document.getElementById('loadingScreen');

function onResLoad(name){
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u2713 '+name;
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

function onResError(name){
  console.warn('Failed to load: '+name+' — using fallback');
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u26a0 '+name+' (fallback)';
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

// Start loading each resource
RESOURCES.forEach(function(r){
  r.img.onload=function(){onResLoad(r.name)};
  r.img.onerror=function(){onResError(r.name)};
  // Set src AFTER attaching handlers to ensure we catch the event
  r.img.src=r.src;
});

// Safety timeout: start game after 8 seconds even if some resources fail
setTimeout(function(){
  if(!gameStarted){
    console.warn('Resource load timeout — starting with fallbacks');
    startGame();
  }
},8000);

var __gameLoopStarted=false;
function startGame(){
  if(gameStarted)return;
  gameStarted=true;
  try{loadBarFill.style.width='100%';if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent='100%';loadText.textContent='Готово';loadDetail.textContent='Мир готов';}catch(_){}
  loadingScreen.style.transition='opacity 0.5s';
  loadingScreen.style.opacity='0';
  setTimeout(function(){
    loadingScreen.style.display='none';
    Promise.resolve(typeof ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap==='function'?ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap():false)
      .then(function(handled){
        if(handled)return;
        var fresh=ppaConsumeFreshStandaloneCharacter();
        if(fresh){
          INV.playerName=fresh.nickname;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=fresh.nickname;
          try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',fresh.nickname)}catch(_){}
          applyClass({name:fresh.className});beginGame();
          return;
        }
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      })
      .catch(function(){
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      });
  },500);
}

// === V219 GATEWAY / CLOUD SAVE BRIDGE ====================================
const PPA_CLOUD={
  ready:false,loading:false,version:null,timer:0,lastErrorAt:0,profile:null
};
function ppaCloudBridgeAvailable(){
  try{return !!(window.PPA&&typeof PPA.isAvailable==='function'&&PPA.isAvailable())}catch(_){return false}
}
function ppaCloudExtract(load){
  var root=load&&typeof load==='object'?load:{};
  var version=root.version;
  if(version==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')version=root.save.version;
  if(version==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')version=root.data.version;
  var state=root.state;
  if(state==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')state=root.save.state!=null?root.save.state:root.save.save;
  if(state==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')state=root.data.state!=null?root.data.state:root.data.save;
  if(state==null&&root.gameSave&&typeof root.gameSave==='object')state=root.gameSave.state||root.gameSave.save;
  if(typeof state==='string'){try{state=JSON.parse(state)}catch(_){state=null}}
  if(!state&&root&&typeof root==='object'&&(Array.isArray(root.bag)||root.equipped))state=root;
  return {version:version==null?null:Number(version),state:state&&typeof state==='object'?state:null};
}
function ppaCloudScheduleSave(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return;
  clearTimeout(PPA_CLOUD.timer);
  var snapshot=state&&typeof state==='object'?state:ppaBuildSaveObject();
  PPA_CLOUD.timer=setTimeout(function(){ppaCloudPush(snapshot)},2500);
}
async function ppaCloudPush(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable()||typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame!=='function')return false;
  try{
    var res=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(state||ppaBuildSaveObject(),PPA_CLOUD.version);
    if(res&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.version);
    else if(res&&res.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.save.version);
    return true;
  }catch(err){
    var now=Date.now();
    if(now-PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt>10000){
      PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt=now;
      console.warn('PPA cloud save:',err);
    }
    return false;
  }
}
function ppaProfileClassKey(profile){
  profile=profile&&typeof profile==='object'?profile:{};
  var raw=profile.classKey||profile.class_key||profile.characterClass||profile.character_class||profile.cls||profile.className||profile.class_name||'';
  raw=String(raw||'').trim();
  var aliases={
    'guardian':'tank','guard':'tank','страж':'tank',
    'paladin':'paladin','паладин':'paladin',
    'berserker':'barbarian','barbarian':'barbarian','берсерк':'barbarian',
    'mage':'mage','маг':'mage',
    'priest':'priest','жрец':'priest',
    'archer':'archer','лучник':'archer',
    'assassin':'assassin','ассасин':'assassin',
    'gnome':'gnome','gunner':'gnome','cannoneer':'gnome','гном-канонир':'gnome','гном канонир':'gnome'
  };
  var low=raw.toLowerCase();
  return aliases[low]||classKeyFromName(raw)||'';
}
function ppaProfileNickname(profile){
  profile=profile&&typeof profile==='object'?profile:{};
  return String(profile.nickname||profile.playerName||profile.player_name||profile.name||'').trim();
}
function ppaShowGatewayError(msg){
  try{
    var fr=document.getElementById('classSelectFrame');

    if(!el){
      el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ppaGatewayError';
