      document.body.appendChild(el);
    }
    el.innerHTML='<div style="max-width:520px;border:1px solid #6b4327;border-radius:12px;padding:18px;background:#160d0c">PHOENIX PIX ARENA<br><br><span style="color:#c9b9a1;font:11px monospace">'+String(msg||'Ошибка Telegram Gateway')+'</span></div>';
  }catch(_){}
}
function ppaMigrationSaveObject(){
  var out=ppaBuildSaveObject();
  var old=(P&&P._saved&&typeof P._saved==='object')?P._saved:null;
  if(old){
    ['lvl','xp','xn','kil','rebirths','statPts','talentPts','cls'].forEach(function(k){
      if(old[k]!=null)out[k]=old[k];
    });
    if(old.statAlloc&&typeof old.statAlloc==='object')out.statAlloc=Object.assign({},old.statAlloc);
    if(old.playerName&&!out.playerName)out.playerName=old.playerName;
  }
  return out;
}

async function ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap(){
  if(!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return false;
  if(PPA_CLOUD.loading)return true;
  PPA_CLOUD.loading=true;
  try{
    if(typeof PPA.ppaAuthTelegram==='function')await PPA.ppaAuthTelegram();
    var profile=typeof PPA.ppaLoadProfile==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadProfile():null;
    profile=(profile&&profile.profile)||profile||{};
    PPA_CLOUD.profile=profile;

    var load=typeof PPA.ppaLoadSave==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadSave():null;
    var cloud=ppaCloudExtract(load);
    PPA_CLOUD.version=cloud.version;

    var forceMigration=localStorage.getItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219')==='1';
    var profileNick=ppaProfileNickname(profile);
    var savedNick=ppaLocalSavedNickname();
    var localCachePresent=ppaHasAnyLocalCharacterCache();
    var cloudHasCharacter=ppaSaveHasCharacterState(cloud.state);

    // Explicit portable-save import always wins: the player deliberately chose it.
    if(forceMigration&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
      var migrationState=ppaMigrationSaveObject();
      if(ppaSaveHasCharacterState(migrationState)){
        var migrated=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(migrationState,cloud.version);
        if(migrated&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.version);
        else if(migrated&&migrated.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.save.version);
        localStorage.removeItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219');
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:migrationState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }
    }

    // Existing cloud character is authoritative for this Telegram account.
    // Apply it directly to memory: no second resource-loading screen.
    if(cloudHasCharacter&&!forceMigration){
      var stamp='

===== applyClass({name #2 @ 109720531 =====
ud character is authoritative for this Telegram account.
    // Apply it directly to memory: no second resource-loading screen.
    if(cloudHasCharacter&&!forceMigration){
      var stamp='v'+String(cloud.version==null?'na':cloud.version);
      if(sessionStorage.getItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp')!==stamp){
        localStorage.setItem('pxSave',JSON.stringify(cloud.state));
        localStorage.setItem('pxSaveLastGood',JSON.stringify(cloud.state));
        if(cloud.state.playerName)try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',String(cloud.state.playerName))}catch(_){}
        sessionStorage.setItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp',stamp);
        if(!loadGame())console.warn('PPA: cloud save could not be applied in memory');
      }
    }

    // Empty cloud + old local cache is the dangerous case. Migrate only when
    // the local nickname belongs to the same profile. Otherwise this is a new
    // Telegram account on a device that still contains another player's cache.
    if(!cloudHasCharacter&&localCachePresent&&!forceMigration){
      var savedNickOk0=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(savedNick);
      var profileNickOk0=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(profileNick);
      if(savedNickOk0&&profileNickOk0&&ppaNickKey(savedNick)===ppaNickKey(profileNick)&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
        var initialState=ppaMigrationSaveObject();
        var first=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(initialState,cloud.version);
        if(first&&Number.isFinite(Number(first.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(first.version);
        else if(first&&first.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(first.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(first.save.version);
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:initialState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }else{
        console.info('PPA: new Telegram account detected; clearing foreign local character cache without reload.');
        ppaClearLocalCharacterCacheForAccountSwitch();
        ppaResetFreshCharacterInMemory(profileNick);
        savedNick=profileNick;
        localCachePresent=false;
      }
    }

    PPA_CLOUD.ready=true;

    // Re-read after possible migration/cleanup decision.
    savedNick=String(
      ((typeof INV!=='undefined'&&INV&&INV.playerName)||
       (P&&P._saved&&P._saved.playerName)||
       window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||
       localStorage.getItem('ppaPlayerNameV205')||'')
    ).trim();
    var savedNickOk=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(savedNick);
    var profileNickOk=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(profileNick);
    var nick=savedNickOk?savedNick:(profileNickOk?profileNick:'');
    if(nick){
      INV.playerName=nick;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=nick;
      try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',nick)}catch(_){}
    }
    // Existing V255 save wins during the one-time Gateway migration.
    // The server reads playerName from its own cloud save, so the client cannot
    // use this path as a free arbitrary rename.
    if(savedNickOk&&savedNick!==profileNick&&typeof PPA.ppaSyncNicknameFromSave==='function'){
      try{
        var syncNick=await PPA.ppaSyncNicknameFromSave();
        if(syncNick&&syncNick.ok&&syncNick.profile)PPA_CLOUD.profile=syncNick.profile;
        else if(syncNick&&syncNick.message)console.warn('PPA nickname migration:',syncNick.message);
      }catch(syncErr){console.warn('PPA nickname migration:',syncErr)}
    }

    var ck=(P._saved&&P._saved.cls)?classKeyFromName(P._saved.cls):'';
    if(!ck)ck=ppaProfileClassKey(profile);
    if(ck&&CLASS_BASE[ck]){
      applyClass({name:CLASS_BASE[ck].name});
      beginGame();
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }catch(err){
    console.error('PPA Gateway bootstrap:',err);
    ppaShowGatewayError('Не удалось подтвердить Telegram-сессию. Закройте Mini App и откройте игру снова через бота.');
    return true;
  }finally{
    PPA_CLOUD.loading=false;
  }
}


// === V263 TEST CLEANUP / PICKAXE PPA ======================================
// Remove ONLY the temporary V260 test items that were tagged v260Test.
// Legitimate player gear/pets are not touched.
function v263RemoveV260TestItems(){
  let changed=false;

  if(INV.equipped&&typeof INV.equipped==='object'){
    Object.keys(INV.equipped).forEach(function(slot){
      const it=INV.equipped[slot];
      if(it&&it.v260Test===true){
        INV.equipped[slot]=null;
        changed=true;
      }
    });
  }

  if(Array.isArray(INV.bag)){
    const before=INV.bag.length;
    INV.bag=INV.bag.filter(function(it){return !(it&&it.v260Test===true)});
    if(INV.bag.length!==before)changed=true;
  }

  if(INV.storage&&typeof INV.storage==='object'){
    ['personal','clan','premium'].forEach(function(key){
      if(!Array.isArray(INV.storage[key]))return;
      const before=INV.storage[key].length;
      INV.storage[key]=INV.storage[key].filter(function(it){return !(it&&it.v260Test===true)});
      if(INV.storage[key].length!==before)changed=true;
    });
  }

  if(changed){
    recomputeStats();
    P.hp=Math.min(P.hp,P.mhp);
    P.mp=Math.min(P.mp,P.mmp);
    saveGame();
    try{
      sendInvState();
      sendBlacksmithState();
      sendStorageState();
      sendPremiumState();
    }catch(_){}
    updateUI();
    showPickup('Временный комплект удалён','#c9b28a');
  }
  return changed;
}

// Keep the earlier requested one-time PPA help for the 200-PPA pickaxe,
// but do not repeatedly top up PPA on every launch.
function v263EnsurePickaxePpa(){
  if(INV.fartPickaxe)return;
  if(INV.v263PickaxePpaGranted)return;
  INV.ppa=Math.max(200,Math.floor(Number(INV.ppa)||0));
  INV.v263PickaxePpaGranted=true;
  saveGame();
  try{sendInvState();sendBlacksmithState();updateUI()}catch(_){}
}

// Exact access rule requested: level 21 through level 40 inclusive.
function fartZoneLevelAllowed(){
  const lvl=Math.floor(Number(P.lvl)||1);
  return lvl>=21&&lvl<=40;
}

// === RELEASE CHARACTER LOAD ================================================
function applyClass(cls){
  if(!cls)return;

  const _classKey=classKeyFromName(cls.name)||'assassin';
  P.cls=CLASS_BASE[_classKey].name;
  P.anim='idle';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;P.attacking=false;
  P.dir8=4;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0;
  PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0;
  cancelSmartAttack();

  // Persistent character only: restore the saved level/progression FIRST,
  // then calculate the selected class base at that level.
  if(P._saved&&P._saved.lvl)P.lvl=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(P._saved.lvl)||1));
  applyClassBaseProfile(_classKey,P.lvl);

  if(P._saved){var sv=P._saved;
    if(sv.lvl)P.lvl=sv.lvl;
    if(sv.xp!=null)P.xp=sv.xp;
    if(sv.kil!=null)P.kil=sv.kil;
    P.xn=xpRequiredForLevel(P.lvl);
    if(typeof sv.statPts==='number')P.statPts=sv.statPts;
    else if(typeof sv.talentPts==='number')P.statPts=sv.talentPts;
    else P.statPts=10+Math.max(0,(P.lvl||1)-1)*3;
    P.statAlloc=Object.assign(
      {hp:0,mp:0,atk:0,def:0,spd:0,atkspd:0,critdmg:0,crit:0,dodge:0},
      sv.statAlloc||{}
    );
    P.rebirths=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(sv.rebirths)||0));
    repairStatPointProgress();
  }

  INV.books.forEach(function(b){
    if(b.applied){
      if(b.id==='fire')P.dmgMul=1.5;
      if(b.id==='ice')P.slowMul=0.7;
      if(b.id==='lightning')P.chain=2;
    }
  });

  P.sp=P.baseSp;
  recomputeStats();
  P.hp=P.mhp;
  P.mp=P.mmp;
  saveGame();
  updateUI();
}

function beginGame(){
  document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='none';
  v263RemoveV260TestItems();
  v263EnsurePickaxePpa();
  if(__gameLoopStarted)return;
  __gameLoopStarted=true;
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
  ln.innerHTML=sc.name+'<div class=\"sub\">'+sc.sub+'</div>';
  ln.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2500);
  PPAOnlineInit();
  let __v178LastLoopErrorAt=0;
  function loop(){
    try{
      update();
    }catch(err){
      const now=Date.now();
      if(now-__v178LastLoopErrorAt>1000){
        __v178LastLoopErrorAt=now;
        console.error('V178 update recovered',err);
      }
      // World-boss rewards must remain playable even if a secondary system
      // throws after the kill. The next frame continues normally.
      try{
        if(P.scene==='worldboss'&&WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active){
          transitioning=false;
          if(P.hp>0)P.dead=false;
          worldBossLootUpdate();
        }
      }catch(_){ }
    }
    try{
      render();
      try{if(window.__PPA_PERF_FRAME)window.__PPA_PERF_FRAME(performance.now())}catch(_){ }
    }catch(err){
      const now=Date.now();
      if(now-__v178LastLoopErrorAt>1000){
        __v178LastLoopErrorAt=now;
        console.error('V178 render recovered',err);
      }
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }
  loop();
}
window.addEventListener('message',function(ev){
  var d=ev.data||{};
  if(d.type==='classChosen'){
    var _nick=String(d.nickname||'').trim();
    if(/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(_nick)){
      // Standalone downloaded-file testing has no Telegram account identity.
      // A different nickname there means a fresh character: never inherit the
      // previous tester/player inventory from the shared file:// localStorage.
      if(!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable()&&ppaHasAnyLocalCharacterCache()){
        var _oldNick=ppaLocalSavedNickname();
        if(!/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(_oldNick)||ppaNickKey(_oldNick)!==ppaNickKey(_nick)){
          // Brand-new local tester/account: clear the previous character and
          // reset the live state right now. Do NOT reload the 100 MB game file.
          ppaClearLocalCharacterCacheForAccountSwitch();
          ppaResetFreshCharacterInMemory(_nick);
        }
      }
      if(typeof INV!=='undefined')INV.playerName=_nick;
      window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=_nick;
      try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',_nick)}catch(_){}
      try{if(typeof PPA_ONLINE!=='undefined')PPA_ONLINE.selfName=_nick}catch(_){}
    }
    applyClass(d.cls);beginGame();
  }
  else if(d.type==='closeChar'){closeInventory();}
  else if(d.type==='exp

===== PPA_PLAYER_NAME #1 @ 98220224 =====
rites||WING_DIR_ART[rarity]||WING_DIR_ART.rare;
  var dir=playerFacing4();

  // Берём стабильный полный спрайт крыльев.
  var fullSrc=(sprites&&sprites.N)||(sprites&&sprites.S)||it.img;
  var im=getCachedImage(fullSrc);
  if(!im||!im.complete||!im.naturalWidth)return;

  var sx=P.x-cam.x;
  var sy=P.y-cam.y;
  var bob=Math.sin(P.bob)*1.8;

  cx.save();
  cx.globalAlpha=.96;
  cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;

  if(dir==='N'){
    // Вверх: оба крыла полностью за спиной.
    var h=76;
    var w=h*(im.naturalWidth/im.naturalHeight);
    w=Math.min(102,Math.max(72,w));
    cx.drawImage(im,sx-w/2,sy-h+26+bob,w,h);
  }
  else if(dir==='S'){
    // Вниз: оба крыла остаются за персонажем.
    // Центральную часть дополнительно перекроет тело персонажа.
    var h=68;
    var w=h*(im.naturalWidth/im.naturalHeight);
    w=Math.min(110,Math.max(84,w));
    cx.drawImage(im,sx-w/2,sy-h+30+bob,w,h);
  }
  else{
    // БОКОВОЙ ВИД:
    // показываем только одно крыло и НЕ разрешаем ему рисоваться внутри центра персонажа.
    var sw=im.naturalWidth/2;
    var sh=im.naturalHeight;
    var h=66;
    var w=h*(sw/sh);
    w=Math.min(50,Math.max(34,w));

    if(dir==='E'){
      // Персонаж бежит вправо:
      // одно крыло полностью слева от корпуса.
      cx.save();
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.rect(-10000,-10000,sx-18+10000,20000);
      cx.clip();
      cx.drawImage(
        im,
        0,0,sw,sh,
        sx-w-18,sy-h+28+bob,w,h
      );
      cx.restore();
    }else{
      // Персонаж бежит влево:
      // одно крыло полностью справа от корпуса.
      cx.save();
      cx.beginPath();
      cx.rect(sx+18,-10000,10000,20000);
      cx.clip();
      cx.drawImage(
        im,
        sw,0,sw,sh,
        sx+18,sy-h+28+bob,w,h
      );
      cx.restore();
    }
  }

  cx.restore();
}
function drawEquippedCloak(){
  var it=INV&&INV.equipped?INV.equipped.cloak:null;if(!it)return;
  var dir=playerFacing4(),sx=P.x-cam.x,sy=P.y-cam.y,c=RCOL_P[it.rarity]||'#777',bob=Math.sin(P.bob)*2;
  cx.save();cx.translate(sx,sy+bob);cx.globalAlpha=.88;cx.fillStyle=c;cx.strokeStyle='rgba(20,15,20,.8)';cx.lineWidth=2;cx.beginPath();
  if(dir==='N'){cx.moveTo(-18,-29);cx.lineTo(18,-29);cx.lineTo(24,25);cx.lineTo(0,37);cx.lineTo(-24,25)}
  else if(dir==='S'){cx.moveTo(-14,-18);cx.lineTo(14,-18);cx.lineTo(18,22);cx.lineTo(0,30);cx.lineTo(-18,22)}
  else if(dir==='E'){cx.moveTo(-8,-24);cx.lineTo(13,-20);cx.lineTo(23,25);cx.lineTo(3,31);cx.lineTo(-10,18)}
  else{cx.moveTo(8,-24);cx.lineTo(-13,-20);cx.lineTo(-23,25);cx.lineTo(-3,31);cx.lineTo(10,18)}
  cx.closePath();cx.fill();cx.stroke();cx.globalAlpha=.45;cx.fillStyle='#fff';cx.beginPath();cx.arc(0,-21,4,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.restore();
}
function drawEquippedArtifact(){
  var it=INV&&INV.equipped?INV.equipped.artifact:null;if(!it)return;
  var im=getCachedImage(it.img||ARTIFACT_ICON_DATA),t=Date.now()*.002,sx=P.x-cam.x+Math.cos(t)*28,sy=P.y-cam.y-36+Math.sin(t*1.3)*7;
  var c=RCOL_P[it.rarity]||'#c990ff';cx.save();cx.globalAlpha=.25;cx.fillStyle=c;cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy,15,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill();cx.globalAlpha=1;
  if(im&&im.complete&&im.naturalWidth>0){cx.imageSmoothingEnabled=false;cx.drawImage(im,sx-12,sy-12,24,24)}
  else{cx.fillStyle=c;cx.beginPath();cx.arc(sx,sy,6,0,Math.PI*2);cx.fill()}cx.restore();
}


// === V204 NICKNAME ABOVE PLAYER ===========================================
function ppaPlayerNickname(){
  try{
    var n=(typeof INV!=='undefined'&&INV&&String(INV.playerName||'').trim())
      ||String(window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'').trim();
    return n||'';
  }catch(_){return String(window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'').trim()}
}
function ppaPlayerVisualTopScreenY(){
  var sy=P.y-cam.y;
  var _sv=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;
  var bob=Math.sin(P.bob)*(playerUsesGnomeSprites()?2.0:3)*_sv;
  var dh,dy;
  if(playerUsesGnomeSprites()){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*GNOME_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(113/128)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesArcherSprites()){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*ARCHER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(184/224)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim]){
    var sc=(P.anim==='idle')?ASSASSIN_IDLE_DRAW_SCALE:(P.anim==='attack'?ASSASSIN_ATTACK_DRAW_SCALE:ASSASSIN_RUN_DRAW_SCALE);
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*sc,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(156/192)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesTankSprites()&&TANK_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*TANK_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(232/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*BERSERKER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(232/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesPriestSprites()&&PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*PRIEST_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(236/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesMageSprites()&&MAGE_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*MAGE_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(236/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*PALADIN_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(234/256)*dh;
  }else{
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*1.4,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-dh;
  }
  return sy+(bob+dy)*_sv;
}
function ppaPlayerClanName(){
  try{
    var c=(typeof CLAN_LOCAL_STATE!=='undefined'&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan)
      ?String(CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan.name||'').trim():'';
    return c;
  }catch(_){return ''}
}
function drawPlayerNickname(){
  var name=ppaPlayerNickname();
  if(!name)return;
  var clan=ppaPlayerClanName();
  var sx=P.x-cam.x;
  var y=ppaPlayerVisualTopScreenY()-9;

  cx.save();
  cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.textBaseline='bottom';
  cx.lineJoin='round';

  if(clan){
    var clanText='['+clan.slice(0,18)+']';
    cx.font='700 8px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
    cx.lineWidth=2.4;
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(8,12,20,.94)';
    cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.78)';
    cx.shadowBlur=3;
    cx.strokeText(clanText,sx,y-12);
    cx.shadowBlur=0;
    cx.fillStyle='#a9cfff';
    cx.fillText(clanText,sx,y-12);
  }

  cx.font='600 10px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
  cx.lineWidth=2.6;
  cx.strokeStyle='rgba(18,8,5,.92)';
  cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.72)';
  cx.shadowBlur=3;
  cx.strokeText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.shadowBlur=0;
  cx.fillStyle='#f2d39a';
  cx.fillText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.restore();
}
// ==========================================================================

function drawPlayer(){
  const sx=P.x-cam.x,sy=P.y-cam.y;
  const siegeVisualScale=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;

  // V277: only the player's 8 classes are drawn 10 px smaller.
  // No collision, camera, NPC, mob, AI or combat values are changed.
  const PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX=10;
  const playerClassDrawHeight=(baseHeight)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterDrawHeight(baseHeight,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const playerClassBodySize=(baseSize)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterBodySize(baseSize,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const isGnome=playerUsesGnomeSprites();
  const isArcher=playerUsesArcherSprites();
  const isAssassinCustom=playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&!!ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isTank=playerUsesTankSprites()&&!!TANK_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isBerserker=playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&!!BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPriest=playerUsesPriestSprites()&&!!PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isMage=playerUsesMageSprites()&&!!MAGE_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPaladin=playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&!!PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim];

// === V204 NICKNAME ABOVE PLAYER ===========================================
function ppaPlayerNickname(){
  try{
    var n=(typeof INV!=='undefined'&&INV&&String(INV.playerName||'').trim())
      ||String(window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'').trim();
    return n||'';
  }catch(_){return String(window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'').trim()}
}
function ppaPlayerVisualTopScreenY(){
  var sy=P.y-cam.y;
  var _sv=P.scene==='clansiege'?CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE:1;
  var bob=Math.sin(P.bob)*(playerUsesGnomeSprites()?2.0:3)*_sv;
  var dh,dy;
  if(playerUsesGnomeSprites()){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*GNOME_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(113/128)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesArcherSprites()){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*ARCHER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(184/224)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim]){
    var sc=(P.anim==='idle')?ASSASSIN_IDLE_DRAW_SCALE:(P.anim==='attack'?ASSASSIN_ATTACK_DRAW_SCALE:ASSASSIN_RUN_DRAW_SCALE);
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*sc,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(156/192)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesTankSprites()&&TANK_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*TANK_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(232/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*BERSERKER_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(232/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesPriestSprites()&&PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*PRIEST_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(236/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesMageSprites()&&MAGE_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*MAGE_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(236/256)*dh;
  }else if(playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim]){
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*PALADIN_DRAW_SCALE,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-(234/256)*dh;
  }else{
    dh=phoneCharacterDrawHeight(P.sz*1.4,_sv);dy=P.sz*.40-dh;
  }
  return sy+(bob+dy)*_sv;
}
function ppaPlayerClanName(){
  try{
    var c=(typeof CLAN_LOCAL_STATE!=='undefined'&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE&&CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan)
      ?String(CLAN_LOCAL_STATE.clan.name||'').trim():'';
    return c;
  }catch(_){return ''}
}
function drawPlayerNickname(){
  var name=ppaPlayerNickname();
  if(!name)return;
  var clan=ppaPlayerClanName();
  var sx=P.x-cam.x;
  var y=ppaPlayerVisualTopScreenY()-9;

  cx.save();
  cx.textAlign='center';
  cx.textBaseline='bottom';
  cx.lineJoin='round';

  if(clan){
    var clanText='['+clan.slice(0,18)+']';
    cx.font='700 8px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
    cx.lineWidth=2.4;
    cx.strokeStyle='rgba(8,12,20,.94)';
    cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.78)';
    cx.shadowBlur=3;
    cx.strokeText(clanText,sx,y-12);
    cx.shadowBlur=0;
    cx.fillStyle='#a9cfff';
    cx.fillText(clanText,sx,y-12);
  }

  cx.font='600 10px Georgia, "Times New Roman", serif';
  cx.lineWidth=2.6;
  cx.strokeStyle='rgba(18,8,5,.92)';
  cx.shadowColor='rgba(0,0,0,.72)';
  cx.shadowBlur=3;
  cx.strokeText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.shadowBlur=0;
  cx.fillStyle='#f2d39a';
  cx.fillText(name.slice(0,18),sx,y);
  cx.restore();
}

function drawPlayer(){

  // No collision, camera, NPC, mob, AI or combat values are changed.
  const PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX=10;
  const playerClassDrawHeight=(baseHeight)=>{
    const sc=Math.max(.01,Number(siegeVisualScale)||1);
    return Math.max(8,phoneCharacterDrawHeight(baseHeight,siegeVisualScale)-PLAYER_CLASS_EXTRA_REDUCTION_PX/sc);
  };
  const playerClassBodySize=(baseSize)=>{

  };
  const isGnome=playerUsesGnomeSprites();
  const isArcher=playerUsesArcherSprites();
  const isAssassinCustom=playerUsesAssassinSprites()&&!!ASSASSIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isTank=playerUsesTankSprites()&&!!TANK_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isBerserker=playerUsesBerserkerSprites()&&!!BERSERKER_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPriest=playerUsesPriestSprites()&&!!PRIEST_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isMage=playerUsesMageSprites()&&!!MAGE_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isPaladin=playerUsesPaladinSprites()&&!!PALADIN_ANIM[P.anim];
  const isCustomDirectional=isGnome||isArcher||isAssassinCustom||isTank||isBerserker||isPriest||isMage||isPaladin;
  const phoneBodyBase=playerClassBodySize(P.sz);

  if(P.scene==='clansiege')cx.scale(CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE,CLAN_SIEGE_PLAYER_VISUAL_SCALE);

    : (isArcher

function unequipSlot(slot){
  var it=INV.equipped[slot];if(!it)return;
  INV.equipped[slot]=null;
  INV.bag.push(it);
  recomputeStats();
}
function sellFromBag(idx){var it=INV.bag[idx];if(!it)return;INV.bag.splice(idx,1);INV.gold+=(it.sell||10);showPickup('+'+(it.sell||10)+' золота','#ffcc44');recomputeStats();updateUI();}
function ppaCanRebuildItemArt(it){
  if(!it||!it.slot)return false;
  if(it.slot==='pet'){
    var pn=it.petName||it.name;
    return !!(pn&&typeof PET_DIR_ART!=='undefined'&&PET_DIR_ART[pn]);
  }
  if(it.slot==='wings'){
    return !!(typeof WING_DIR_ART!=='undefined'&&(WING_DIR_ART[it.rarity]||WING_DIR_ART.rare));
  }
  if(it.slot==='cloak'||it.slot==='artifact')return true;
  if(it.slot==='necklace')return true;
  return !!(typeof CLASS_ART_SLOTS!=='undefined'&&CLASS_ART_SLOTS[it.slot]&&it.classKey&&it.classKey!=='all');
}
function ppaCompactItemForSave(it){
  if(!it||typeof it!=='object')return it;
  var o=Object.assign({},it);
  if(ppaCanRebuildItemArt(o)){
    delete o.img;
    delete o.dirSprites;
    delete o.cardArt;
    delete o.iconArt;
  }
  return o;
}
function ppaCompactListForSave(a){
  return Array.isArray(a)?a.map(ppaCompactItemForSave):[];
}
function ppaCompactStorageForSave(st){
  st=st||{};
  return {
    personal:ppaCompactListForSave(st.personal),
    clan:ppaCompactListForSave(st.clan),
    premium:ppaCompactListForSave(st.premium)
  };
}
function ppaCompactEquippedForSave(eq){
  var o={};eq=eq||{};
  Object.keys(eq).forEach(function(k){o[k]=eq[k]?ppaCompactItemForSave(eq[k]):null});
  return o;
}
function ppaCompactAuctionPayloadForSave(it){
  if(!it||typeof it!=='object')return it;
  var o=Object.assign({},it);
  delete o.img;delete o.cardArt;delete o.iconArt;delete o.dirSprites;
  if(o.gear)o.gear=ppaCompactItemForSave(o.gear);
  return o;
}
function ppaCompactAuctionLotsForSave(a){
  return Array.isArray(a)?a.map(function(l){
    if(!l||typeof l!=='object')return l;
    var x=Object.assign({},l);x.item=ppaCompactAuctionPayloadForSave(l.item);return x;
  }):[];
}
function ppaHydrateItemArt(it){
  if(!it)return it;
  refreshGearArt(it);
  if(it.slot==='pet'){
    var pn=it.petName||it.name,ps=(typeof PET_DIR_ART!=='undefined'&&pn)?PET_DIR_ART[pn]:null;
    if(ps){it.dirSprites=ps;it.img=(ps.S||ps.SE||ps.E||it.img||'')}
  }else if(it.slot==='wings'){
    var ws=(typeof WING_DIR_ART!=='undefined')?(WING_DIR_ART[it.rarity]||WING_DIR_ART.rare):null;
    if(ws){it.dirSprites=ws;it.img=(ws.S||ws.SE||ws.E||it.img||'')}
  }else if(it.slot==='cloak'&&typeof CLOAK_ICON_DATA!=='undefined'){
    it.img=accessoryIconForRarity('cloak',it.rarity)||it.img||'';
  }else if(it.slot==='artifact'&&typeof ARTIFACT_ICON_DATA!=='undefined'){
    it.img=accessoryIconForRarity('artifact',it.rarity)||it.img||'';
  }
  return it;
}

    lvl:P.lvl,xp:P.xp,xn:P.xn,kil:P.kil,rebirths:P.rebirths||0,statPts:P.statPts,statAlloc:P.statAlloc,

  };
}
function ppaSaveLooksRich(o){
  if(!o||typeof o!=='object')return false;
  if(Array.isArray(o.bag)&&o.bag.length)return true;
  if(o.equipped&&Object.keys(o.equipped).some(function(k){return !!o.equipped[k]}))return true;
  if(o.storage&&Object.keys(o.storage).some(function(k){return Array.isArray(o.storage[k])&&o.storage[k].length}))return true;
  if(o.grimoires&&Object.keys(o.grimoires).some(function(k){return Number(o.grimoires[k])>0}))return true;
  if(o.skillRanks&&Object.keys(o.skillRanks).some(function(k){return Number(o.skillRanks[k])>0}))return true;
  if(Array.isArray(o.books)&&o.books.some(function(b){return b&&b.applied}))return true;
  return false;
}

// V257 account isolation: a valid character save may legitimately have an empty
// bag (for example a brand-new player or someone who sold/moved all gear).
// playerName/progression/currency make it an owned character state too.
function ppaSaveHasCharacterState(o){
  if(!o||typeof o!=='object')return false;
  if(ppaSaveLooksRich(o))return true;
  if(/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(String(o.playerName||'').trim()))return true;
  if(Number(o.lvl)>1||Number(o.xp)>0||Number(o.kil)>0||Number(o.rebirths)>0)return true;
  if(Number(o.gold)>0||Number(o.gram)>0||Number(o.ppa)>0||Number(o.arenaTokens)>0||Number(o.renameCards)>0)return true;
  if(o.materials&&Object.keys(o.materials).some(function(k){return Number(o.materials[k])>0}))return true;
  if(o.stones&&Object.keys(o.stones).some(function(k){return Number(o.stones[k])>0}))return true;
  if(o.runes&&Object.keys(o.runes).some(function(k){return Number(o.runes[k])>0}))return true;
  return false;
}
function ppaNickKey(v){return String(v||'').trim().toLocaleLowerCase('ru-RU')}
function ppaLocalSavedNickname(){
  try{
    var raw=localStorage.getItem('pxSave')||localStorage.getItem('pxSaveLastGood')||'';
    var s=raw?JSON.parse(raw):null;
    return String((s&&s.playerName)||localStorage.getItem('ppaPlayerNameV205')||'').trim();
  }catch(_){return String(localStorage.getItem('ppaPlayerNameV205')||'').trim()}
}

  try{
    return !!(
      localStorage.getItem('pxSave')||localStorage.getItem('pxSaveLastGood')||
      localStorage.getItem('pxSaveCleanV251')||localStorage.getItem('pxSaveLastGoodCleanV251')||
      localStorage.getItem('pxSaveCleanV251LastGoodCleanV251')
    );
  }catch(_){return false}
}
function ppaClearLocalCharacterCacheForAccountSwitch(){
  var keys=[
    'pxSave','pxSaveLastGood','ppaPlayerNameV205','ppaAiTrainingV174',
    'pxArenaBestWave','pxArenaShopDailyV52','pxBossAt','pxPvpDailyV51','pxPvpRatingV52','pxPvpHistoryV59',
    'pxSaveCleanV251','pxSaveCleanV251LastGoodCleanV251','pxSaveLastGoodCleanV251',
    'pxArenaBestWaveCleanV251','pxArenaShopDailyV52CleanV251','pxBossAtCleanV251',
    'pxPvpDailyV51CleanV251','pxPvpRatingV52CleanV251','ppaPlayerNameV205CleanV251','ppaAiTrainingV174CleanV251'
  ];
  try{keys.forEach(function(k){localStorage.removeItem(k)})}catch(_){}
  try{sessionStorage.removeItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp')}catch(_){}
}

function ppaResetFreshCharacterInMemory(nickname){
  try{
    var freshInv=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(PPA_FRESH_INV_TEMPLATE));
    Object.keys(freshInv).forEach(function(k){INV[k]=freshInv[k]});
    var freshP=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(PPA_FRESH_PLAYER_TEMPLATE));
    Object.keys(freshP).forEach(function(k){P[k]=freshP[k]});
    try{delete P._saved}catch(_){P._saved=null}

    P.scene='safe';
    P.x=SCENES.safe.startPos.x;
    P.y=SCENES.safe.startPos.y;
    P.vx=0;P.vy=0;P.dead=false;P.tid=null;
    transitioning=false;

    try{EN.length=0}catch(_){}
    try{PR.length=0}catch(_){}
    try{PT.length=0}catch(_){}
    try{LOOT.length=0}catch(_){}
    try{PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0}catch(_){}
    try{PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0}catch(_){}

    var nick=String(nickname||'').trim();
    INV.playerName=nick;
    window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=nick;
    try{
      if(nick)localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',nick);
      else localStorage.removeItem('ppaPlayerNameV205');
    }catch(_){}
    return true;
  }catch(err){
    console.warn('PPA fresh in-memory reset failed:',err);
    return false;
  }
}

function ppaQueueFreshStandaloneCharacter(nickname,className){
  ppaClearLocalCharacterCacheForAccountSwitch();
  // Android content:// may recreate sessionStorage on a document reload.
  // Keep this ONE-SHOT handoff in localStorage instead, then consume/remove it
  // immediately after the clean reload. This prevents the first-start loop.
  try{
    localStorage.setItem('ppaFreshStandaloneCharacterV257',JSON.stringify({
      nickname:String(nickname||''),
      className:String(className||'')
    }));
    return true;
  }catch(_){return false}
}
function ppaConsumeFreshStandaloneCharacter(){
  try{
    var raw=localStorage.getItem('ppaFreshStandaloneCharacterV257');
    if(!raw)return null;
    localStorage.removeItem('ppaFreshStandaloneCharacterV257');
    var x=JSON.parse(raw);
    if(!x||!x.className||!/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(String(x.nickname||'').trim()))return null;
    return {nickname:String(x.nickname).trim(),className:String(x.className)};
  }catch(_){
    try{localStorage.removeItem('ppaFreshStandaloneCharacterV257')}catch(__){}
    return null;
  }
}
var PPA_V239_LAST_GOOD_WRITE=0;
function saveGame(){
  try{
    var next=ppaBuildSaveObject(),rich=ppaSaveHasCharacterState(next),nextRaw=JSON.stringify(next);
    if(!rich){
      var prevRaw=localStorage.getItem('pxSave'),prev=null;
      try{prev=prevRaw?JSON.parse(prevRaw):null}catch(_){}
      if(prev&&ppaSaveHasCharacterState(prev)){
        localStorage.setItem('pxSaveLastGood',prevRaw);
        return false;
      }
    }
    localStorage.setItem('pxSave',nextRaw);
    var now=Date.now();
    if(rich&&(now-PPA_V239_LAST_GOOD_WRITE>15000||!localStorage.getItem('pxSaveLastGood'))){
      localStorage.setItem('pxSaveLastGood',nextRaw);
      PPA_V239_LAST_GOOD_WRITE=now;
    }
    try{ppaCloudScheduleSave(next)}catch(_){}
    return true;
  }catch(e){return false}
}

const PPA_SAVE_EXPORT_KEYS=['pxSave','pxSaveLastGood','pxArenaBestWave','pxArenaShopDailyV52','pxBossAt','pxPvpDailyV51','pxPvpRatingV52'];
function ppaExportSave(){
  try{
    var data={format:'PPA_PORTABLE_SAVE_V1',createdAt:new Date().toISOString(),values:{}};
    PPA_SAVE_EXPORT_KEYS.forEach(function(k){
      var v=localStorage.getItem(k);
      if(v!==null)data.values[k]=v;
    });
    var blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(data,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
    var a=document.createElement('a');
    var u=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    a.href=u;
    a.download='PPA_SAVE_BACKUP_'+new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10)+'.json';
    document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();
    setTimeout(function(){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(u)}catch(_){}},1000);
    showPickup('СЕЙВ ЭКСПОРТИРОВАН','#7dff9f');
    return true;
  }catch(e){showPickup('НЕ УДАЛОСЬ ЭКСПОРТИРОВАТЬ СЕЙВ','#ff7777');return false}
}
function ppaImportSaveText(txt){
  try{
    var x=JSON.parse(String(txt||''));
    if(x&&x.format==='PPA_PORTABLE_SAVE_V1'&&x.values){
      Object.keys(x.values).forEach(function(k){
        if(PPA_SAVE_EXPORT_KEYS.indexOf(k)>=0&&typeof x.values[k]==='string')localStorage.setItem(k,x.values[k]);
      });
    }else if(x&&typeof x==='object'&&(Array.isArray(x.bag)||x.equipped)){
      localStorage.setItem('pxSave',JSON.stringify(x));
      if(ppaSaveLooksRich(x))localStorage.setItem('pxSaveLastGood',JSON.stringify(x));
    }else throw new Error('bad save');
    // Mark an explicit owner migration. On the next Telegram/Lovable start,
    // this imported local save wins over an already-created empty cloud slot.
    localStorage.setItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219','1');
    showPickup('СЕЙВ ВОССТАНОВЛЕН · ПЕРЕЗАГРУЗКА','#7dff9f');
    setTimeout(function(){location.reload()},450);
    return true;
  }catch(e){showPickup('ФАЙЛ СЕЙВА НЕ РАСПОЗНАН','#ff7777');return false}
}
window.PPA_EXPORT_SAVE=ppaExportSave;
window.PPA_IMPORT_SAVE_TEXT=ppaImportSaveText;

// Mobile combat save debounce: keep combat smooth and flush as soon as action calms down.
let PPA_COMBAT_SAVE_TIMER=0,PPA_COMBAT_SAVE_DIRTY=false;
function scheduleCombatSave(){
  PPA_COMBAT_SAVE_DIRTY=true;
  if(PPA_COMBAT_SAVE_TIMER)clearTimeout(PPA_COMBAT_SAVE_TIMER);
  PPA_COMBAT_SAVE_TIMER=setTimeout(function(){
    PPA_COMBAT_SAVE_TIMER=0;
    if(PPA_COMBAT_SAVE_DIRTY){PPA_COMBAT_SAVE_DIRTY=false;saveGame()}
  },1400);
}
function flushCombatSave(){
  if(PPA_COMBAT_SAVE_TIMER){clearTimeout(PPA_COMBAT_SAVE_TIMER);PPA_COMBAT_SAVE_TIMER=0}
  if(PPA_COMBAT_SAVE_DIRTY){PPA_COMBAT_SAVE_DIRTY=false;saveGame()}
}
window.addEventListener('beforeunload',flushCombatSave);
document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',function(){if(document.hidden)flushCombatSave()});

function ppaMigrateIsolatedSandboxSaveOnce(){
  try{
    const pairs=[
      ['pxSaveCleanV251','pxSave'],
      ['pxSaveCleanV251LastGoodCleanV251','pxSaveLastGood'],
      ['pxSaveLastGoodCleanV251','pxSaveLastGood'],
      ['pxArenaBestWaveCleanV251','pxArenaBestWave'],
      ['pxArenaShopDailyV52CleanV251','pxArenaShopDailyV52'],
      ['pxBossAtCleanV251','pxBossAt'],
      ['pxPvpDailyV51CleanV251','pxPvpDailyV51'],
      ['pxPvpRatingV52CleanV251','pxPvpRatingV52'],
      ['ppaPlayerNameV205CleanV251','ppaPlayerNameV205'],
      ['ppaAiTrainingV174CleanV251','ppaAiTrainingV174']
    ];
    pairs.forEach(function(p){
      if(localStorage.getItem(p[1])!==null)return;
      const v=localStorage.getItem(p[0]);
      if(v!==null)localStorage.setItem(p[1],v);
    });
  }catch(_){}
}

function loadGame(){try{
  var raw=localStorage.getItem('pxSave')||localStorage.getItem('pxSaveLastGood')||'';
  var s=JSON.parse(raw||'null');if(!s)return false;
  if(!localStorage.getItem('pxSave'))localStorage.setItem('pxSave',JSON.stringify(s));
  INV.bag=Array.isArray(s.bag)?s.bag:[];
  INV.storage=(s.storage&&typeof s.storage==='object')?s.storage:{personal:[],clan:[],premium:[]};
  ['personal','clan','premium'].forEach(function(k){if(!Array.isArray(INV.storage[k]))INV.storage[k]=[];});
  INV.auctionLots=Array.isArray(s.auctionLots)?s.auctionLots:[];
  INV.blackMarket=(s.blackMarket&&typeof s.blackMarket==='object')?s.blackMarket:null;
  INV.clanStorageMeta=(s.clanStorageMeta&&typeof s.clanStorageMeta==='object')?s.clanStorageMeta:{permissions:{},history:[]};
  if(!INV.clanStorageMeta.permissions||typeof INV.clanStorageMeta.permissions!=='object')INV.clanStorageMeta.permissions={};
  if(!Array.isArray(INV.clanStorageMeta.history))INV.clanStorageMeta.history=[];
  INV.clanLocalData=(s.clanLocalData&&typeof s.clanLocalData==='object')?s.clanLocalData:null;
  INV.clanJoinBlockedUntil=Math.max(0,Number(s.clanJoinBlockedUntil)||0);
  INV.v76ClanResetDone=!!s.v76ClanResetDone;
  INV.v77CooldownResetDone=!!s.v77CooldownResetDone;

  // V77 requested one-time cooldown reset:
  // clear only the CURRENT 24h lock once, without disabling the mechanic.
  if(!INV.v77CooldownResetDone){
    INV.clanJoinBlockedUntil=0;
    INV.v77CooldownResetDone=true;
  }

  // V76 legacy clan reset. This clears ONLY the old local prototype clan once.
  // It does not create the 24h cooldown and never touches a future server clan.
  if(!INV.v76ClanResetDone){
    INV.clanLocalData=null;
    INV.clanStorageMeta={permissions:{},history:[]};

    // Preserve any old clan-storage items instead of deleting them.
    if(INV.storage&&Array.isArray(INV.storage.clan)&&INV.storage.clan.length){
      if(!Array.isArray(INV.storage.personal))INV.storage.personal=[];
      if(!Array.isArray(INV.storage.premium))INV.storage.premium=[];
      var _recover=INV.storage.clan.splice(0);
      _recover.forEach(function(_it){
        if(INV.storage.personal.length<200)INV.storage.personal.push(_it);
        else if((INV.bag||[]).length<100)INV.bag.push(_it);
        else if(INV.storage.premium.length<50)INV.storage.premium.push(_it);
        else INV.storage.clan.push(_it);
      });
    }

    INV.clanJoinBlockedUntil=0;
    INV.v76ClanResetDone=true;
  }
  if(typeof s.gramSpentLifetime==='number')INV.gramSpentLifetime=Math.max(0,s.gramSpentLifetime);
  else INV.gramSpentLifetime=0;
  // V122: slots come from VERIFIED lifetime paid Gram, not from Gram spent in-game.
  INV.lifetimePaidGram=typeof s.lifetimePaidGram==='number'?Math.max(0,s.lifetimePaidGram):0;
  INV.equipped=s.equipped||{weapon:null,helmet:null,armor:null,gloves:null,ring:null,legs:null,boots:null,artifact:null,necklace:null,cloak:null,wings:null,pet:null};
  if(!('cloak' in INV.equipped))INV.equipped.cloak=null;if(!('wings' in INV.equipped))INV.equipped.wings=null;if(!('pet' in INV.equipped))INV.equipped.pet=null;
  EQUIP_SLOTS.forEach(function(k){if(!(k in INV.equipped))INV.equipped[k]=null;});
  INV.playerName=String(s.playerName||localStorage.getItem('ppaPlayerNameV205')||'').trim();
  if(INV.playerName){
    window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=INV.playerName;
    try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',INV.playerName)}catch(_){}
  }
  INV.renameCards=(typeof s.renameCards==='number')?Math.max(0,Math.floor(s.renameCards)):0;
  if(typeof s.gold==='number')INV.gold=s.gold;
  if(typeof s.gram==='number')INV.gram=s.gram;
  if(typeof s.ppa==='number')INV.ppa=s.ppa;
  INV.fartPickaxe=!!s.fartPickaxe;
  INV.fartGuardCooldowns=(s.fartGuardCooldowns&&typeof s.fartGuardCooldowns==='object')?s.fartGuardCooldowns:{};
  INV.v263PickaxePpaGranted=!!s.v263PickaxePpaGranted;
  INV.arenaTokens=typeof s.arenaTokens==='number'?Math.max(0,Math.floor(s.arenaTokens)):0;
  INV.aiChallenge=(s.aiChallenge&&typeof s.aiChallenge==='object')?s.aiChallenge:null;
  INV.worldBossDaily=(s.worldBossDaily&&typeof s.worldBossDaily==='object')?s.worldBossDaily:null;
  INV.clanSiegeReward=(s.clanSiegeReward&&typeof s.clanSiegeReward==='object')?Object.assign({clanKey:'',clanName:'',capturedAt:0,expiresAt:0,xpPct:5,goldPct:5,resourcePct:5,movePct:3},s.clanSiegeReward):{clanKey:'',clanName:'',capturedAt:0,expiresAt:0,xpPct:5,goldPct:5,resourcePct:5,movePct:3};
  INV.arenaXpDaily=(s.arenaXpDaily&&typeof s.arenaXpDaily==='object')?Object.assign({day:'',used:0},s.arenaXpDaily):{day:'',used:0};
  INV.dungeon21LordReadyAt=Math.max(0,Number(s.dungeon21LordReadyAt)||0);
  INV.dungeonEliteState=(s.dungeonEliteState&&typeof s.dungeonEliteState==='object')
    ?Object.assign({one20Killed:'',twenty40Killed:''},s.dungeonEliteState)
    :{one20Killed:'',twenty40Killed:''};
  INV.grimoireRankDrops=(s.grimoireRankDrops&&typeof s.grimoireRankDrops==='object')?s.grimoireRankDrops:{};
  INV.clanCoins=typeof s.clanCoins==='number'?Math.max(0,Math.floor(s.clanCoins)):0;
  INV.materials=(s.materials&&typeof s.materials==='object')?s.materials:{};
  INV.feathers=(s.feathers&&typeof s.feathers==='object')?s.feathers:{phoenix:0};
  if(typeof INV.feathers.phoenix!=='number')INV.feathers.phoenix=0;
  INV.stones=Object.assign({normal:0,premium:0,rune:0},s.stones||{});
  INV.runes=(s.runes&&typeof s.runes==='object')?s.runes:{};
  INV.runeSlots=Array.isArray(s.runeSlots)?s.runeSlots.slice(0,RUNE_MAX_SLOTS):Array(RUNE_MAX_SLOTS).fill(null);
  INV.runeProgress=(s.runeProgress&&typeof s.runeProgress==='object')?s.runeProgress:{levelSlots:0,rebirthSlots:0};
  normalizeRuneState();
  INV.grimoires=(s.grimoires&&typeof s.grimoires==='object')?s.grimoires:{};
  INV.skillRanks=(s.skillRanks&&typeof s.skillRanks==='object')?s.skillRanks:{};
  function normalizeLoadedItem(it){
    if(!it)return;
    // V210: stackable storage entries (materials, potions, stones, scrolls, grimoires) are not gear.
    if(it.kind&&it.kind!=='gear'&&!it.slot){
      it.count=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(it.count)||1));
      return;
    }
    if(typeof it.enh!=='number')it.enh=0;
    if('lvl' in it)delete it.lvl;

let loadedCount=0;
const totalRes=RESOURCES.length;
let gameStarted=false;
const loadBarFill=document.getElementById('loadBarFill');
const loadText=document.getElementById('loadText');
const loadDetail=document.getElementById('loadDetail');
const loadPct=document.getElementById('loadPct');
const loadingScreen=document.getElementById('loadingScreen');

function onResLoad(name){
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u2713 '+name;
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

function onResError(name){
  console.warn('Failed to load: '+name+' — using fallback');
  loadedCount++;
  const pct=Math.round((loadedCount/totalRes)*100);
  loadBarFill.style.width=pct+'%';
  if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent=pct+'%';
  loadText.textContent='\u0417\u0430\u0433\u0440\u0443\u0437\u043a\u0430... '+loadedCount+'/'+totalRes+' ('+pct+'%)';
  loadDetail.textContent='\u26a0 '+name+' (fallback)';
  if(loadedCount>=totalRes && !gameStarted){
    startGame();
  }
}

// Start loading each resource
RESOURCES.forEach(function(r){
  r.img.onload=function(){onResLoad(r.name)};
  r.img.onerror=function(){onResError(r.name)};
  // Set src AFTER attaching handlers to ensure we catch the event
  r.img.src=r.src;
});

// Safety timeout: start game after 8 seconds even if some resources fail
setTimeout(function(){
  if(!gameStarted){
    console.warn('Resource load timeout — starting with fallbacks');
    startGame();
  }
},8000);

var __gameLoopStarted=false;
function startGame(){
  if(gameStarted)return;
  gameStarted=true;
  try{loadBarFill.style.width='100%';if(loadPct)loadPct.textContent='100%';loadText.textContent='Готово';loadDetail.textContent='Мир готов';}catch(_){}
  loadingScreen.style.transition='opacity 0.5s';
  loadingScreen.style.opacity='0';
  setTimeout(function(){
    loadingScreen.style.display='none';
    Promise.resolve(typeof ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap==='function'?ppaReleaseGatewayBootstrap():false)
      .then(function(handled){
        if(handled)return;
        var fresh=ppaConsumeFreshStandaloneCharacter();
        if(fresh){
          INV.playerName=fresh.nickname;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=fresh.nickname;
          try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',fresh.nickname)}catch(_){}
          applyClass({name:fresh.className});beginGame();
          return;
        }
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      })
      .catch(function(){
        document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='block';
      });
  },500);
}

// === V219 GATEWAY / CLOUD SAVE BRIDGE ====================================
const PPA_CLOUD={
  ready:false,loading:false,version:null,timer:0,lastErrorAt:0,profile:null
};
function ppaCloudBridgeAvailable(){
  try{return !!(window.PPA&&typeof PPA.isAvailable==='function'&&PPA.isAvailable())}catch(_){return false}
}
function ppaCloudExtract(load){
  var root=load&&typeof load==='object'?load:{};
  var version=root.version;
  if(version==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')version=root.save.version;
  if(version==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')version=root.data.version;
  var state=root.state;
  if(state==null&&root.save&&typeof root.save==='object')state=root.save.state!=null?root.save.state:root.save.save;
  if(state==null&&root.data&&typeof root.data==='object')state=root.data.state!=null?root.data.state:root.data.save;
  if(state==null&&root.gameSave&&typeof root.gameSave==='object')state=root.gameSave.state||root.gameSave.save;
  if(typeof state==='string'){try{state=JSON.parse(state)}catch(_){state=null}}
  if(!state&&root&&typeof root==='object'&&(Array.isArray(root.bag)||root.equipped))state=root;
  return {version:version==null?null:Number(version),state:state&&typeof state==='object'?state:null};
}
function ppaCloudScheduleSave(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return;
  clearTimeout(PPA_CLOUD.timer);
  var snapshot=state&&typeof state==='object'?state:ppaBuildSaveObject();
  PPA_CLOUD.timer=setTimeout(function(){ppaCloudPush(snapshot)},2500);
}
async function ppaCloudPush(state){
  if(!PPA_CLOUD.ready||!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable()||typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame!=='function')return false;
  try{
    var res=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(state||ppaBuildSaveObject(),PPA_CLOUD.version);
    if(res&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.version);
    else if(res&&res.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(res.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(res.save.version);
    return true;
  }catch(err){
    var now=Date.now();
    if(now-PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt>10000){
      PPA_CLOUD.lastErrorAt=now;
      console.warn('PPA cloud save:',err);
    }
    return false;
  }
}
function ppaProfileClassKey(profile){
  profile=profile&&typeof profile==='object'?profile:{};
  var raw=profile.classKey||profile.class_key||profile.characterClass||profile.character_class||profile.cls||profile.className||profile.class_name||'';
  raw=String(raw||'').trim();
  var aliases={
    'guardian':'tank','guard':'tank','страж':'tank',
    'paladin':'paladin','паладин':'paladin',
    'berserker':'barbarian','barbarian':'barbarian','берсерк':'barbarian',
    'mage':'mage','маг':'mage',
    'priest':'priest','жрец':'priest',
    'archer':'archer','лучник':'archer',
    'assassin':'assassin','ассасин':'assassin',
    'gnome':'gnome','gunner':'gnome','cannoneer':'gnome','гном-канонир':'gnome','гном канонир':'gnome'
  };
  var low=raw.toLowerCase();
  return aliases[low]||classKeyFromName(raw)||'';
}
function ppaProfileNickname(profile){
  profile=profile&&typeof profile==='object'?profile:{};
  return String(profile.nickname||profile.playerName||profile.player_name||profile.name||'').trim();
}
function ppaShowGatewayError(msg){
  try{
    var fr=document.getElementById('classSelectFrame');
    if(fr)fr.style.display='none';
    var el=document.getElementById('ppaGatewayError');
    if(!el){
      el=document.createElement('div');el.id='ppaGatewayError';
      el.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:10050;display:grid;place-items:center;padding:24px;background:#090708;color:#e7c98d;font:700 14px/1.5 Georgia,serif;text-align:center';
      document.body.appendChild(el);
    }
    el.innerHTML='<div style="max-width:520px;border:1px solid #6b4327;border-radius:12px;padding:18px;background:#160d0c">PHOENIX PIX ARENA<br><br><span style="color:#c9b9a1;font:11px monospace">'+String(msg||'Ошибка Telegram Gateway')+'</span></div>';
  }catch(_){}
}
function ppaMigrationSaveObject(){
  var out=ppaBuildSaveObject();
  var old=(P&&P._saved&&typeof P._saved==='object')?P._saved:null;
  if(old){
    ['lvl','xp','xn','kil','rebirths','statPts','talentPts','cls'].forEach(function(k){
      if(old[k]!=null)out[k]=old[k];
    });
    if(old.statAlloc&&typeof old.statAlloc==='object')out.statAlloc=Object.assign({},old.statAlloc);
    if(old.playerName&&!out.playerName)out.playerName=old.playerName;
  }
  return out;
}

  if(!ppaCloudBridgeAvailable())return false;
  if(PPA_CLOUD.loading)return true;
  PPA_CLOUD.loading=true;
  try{
    if(typeof PPA.ppaAuthTelegram==='function')await PPA.ppaAuthTelegram();

    profile=(profile&&profile.profile)||profile||{};
    PPA_CLOUD.profile=profile;

    var load=typeof PPA.ppaLoadSave==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadSave():null;
    var cloud=ppaCloudExtract(load);
    PPA_CLOUD.version=cloud.version;

    var forceMigration=localStorage.getItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219')==='1';
    var profileNick=ppaProfileNickname(profile);
    var savedNick=ppaLocalSavedNickname();
    var localCachePresent=ppaHasAnyLocalCharacterCache();
    var cloudHasCharacter=ppaSaveHasCharacterState(cloud.state);

    // Explicit portable-save import always wins: the player deliberately chose it.
    if(forceMigration&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
      var migrationState=ppaMigrationSaveObject();
      if(ppaSaveHasCharacterState(migrationState)){
        var migrated=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(migrationState,cloud.version);
        if(migrated&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.version);
        else if(migrated&&migrated.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.save.version);
        localStorage.removeItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219');
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:migrationState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }
    }

    // Existing cloud character is authoritative for this Telegram account.
    // A

ile;

    var load=typeof PPA.ppaLoadSave==='function'?await PPA.ppaLoadSave():null;
    var cloud=ppaCloudExtract(load);
    PPA_CLOUD.version=cloud.version;

    var forceMigration=localStorage.getItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219')==='1';
    var profileNick=ppaProfileNickname(profile);
    var savedNick=ppaLocalSavedNickname();
    var localCachePresent=ppaHasAnyLocalCharacterCache();
    var cloudHasCharacter=ppaSaveHasCharacterState(cloud.state);

    // Explicit portable-save import always wins: the player deliberately chose it.
    if(forceMigration&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
      var migrationState=ppaMigrationSaveObject();
      if(ppaSaveHasCharacterState(migrationState)){
        var migrated=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(migrationState,cloud.version);
        if(migrated&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.version);
        else if(migrated&&migrated.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(migrated.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(migrated.save.version);
        localStorage.removeItem('ppaCloudForceMigrationV219');
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:migrationState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }
    }

    // Existing cloud character is authoritative for this Telegram account.
    // Apply it directly to memory: no second resource-loading screen.
    if(cloudHasCharacter&&!forceMigration){
      var stamp='v'+String(cloud.version==null?'na':cloud.version);
      if(sessionStorage.getItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp')!==stamp){
        localStorage.setItem('pxSave',JSON.stringify(cloud.state));
        localStorage.setItem('pxSaveLastGood',JSON.stringify(cloud.state));
        if(cloud.state.playerName)try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',String(cloud.state.playerName))}catch(_){}
        sessionStorage.setItem('ppaCloudLoadedStamp',stamp);
        if(!loadGame())console.warn('PPA: cloud save could not be applied in memory');
      }
    }

    // Empty cloud + old local cache is the dangerous case. Migrate only when
    // the local nickname belongs to the same profile. Otherwise this is a new
    // Telegram account on a device that still contains another player's cache.
    if(!cloudHasCharacter&&localCachePresent&&!forceMigration){
      var savedNickOk0=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(savedNick);
      var profileNickOk0=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(profileNick);
      if(savedNickOk0&&profileNickOk0&&ppaNickKey(savedNick)===ppaNickKey(profileNick)&&typeof PPA.ppaSaveGame==='function'){
        var initialState=ppaMigrationSaveObject();
        var first=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(initialState,cloud.version);
        if(first&&Number.isFinite(Number(first.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(first.version);
        else if(first&&first.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(first.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(first.save.version);
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:initialState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }else{
        console.info('PPA: new Telegram account detected; clearing foreign local character cache without reload.');
        ppaClearLocalCharacterCacheForAccountSwitch();
        ppaResetFreshCharacterInMemory(profileNick);
        savedNick=profileNick;
        localCachePresent=false;
      }
    }

    PPA_CLOUD.ready=true;

    // Re-read after possible migration/cleanup decision.
    savedNick=String(
      ((typeof INV!=='undefined'&&INV&&INV.playerName)||
       (P&&P._saved&&P._saved.playerName)||
       window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||
       localStorage.getItem('ppaPlayerNameV205')||'')
    ).trim();
    var savedNickOk=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(savedNick);
    var profileNickOk=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(profileNick);
    var nick=savedNickOk?savedNick:(profileNickOk?profileNick:'');
    if(nick){
      INV.playerName=nick;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=nick;
      try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',nick)}catch(_){}
    }
    // Existing V255 save wins during the one-time Gateway migration.
    // The server reads playerName from its own cloud save, so the client cannot
    // use this path as a free arbitrary rename.
    if(savedNickOk&&savedNick!==profileNick&&typeof PPA.ppaSyncNicknameFromSave==='function'){
      try{
        var syncNick=await PPA.ppaSyncNicknameFromSave();
        if(syncNick&&syncNick.ok&&syncNick.profile)PPA_CLOUD.profile=syncNick.profile;
        else if(syncNick&&syncNick.message)console.warn('PPA nickname migration:',syncNick.message);
      }catch(syncErr){console.warn('PPA nickname migration:',syncErr)}
    }

    var ck=(P._saved&&P._saved.cls)?classKeyFromName(P._saved.cls):'';
    if(!ck)ck=ppaProfileClassKey(profile);
    if(ck&&CLASS_BASE[ck]){
      applyClass({name:CLASS_BASE[ck].name});
      beginGame();
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }catch(err){
    console.error('PPA Gateway bootstrap:',err);
    ppaShowGatewayError('Не удалось подтвердить Telegram-сессию. Закройте Mini App и откройте игру снова через бота.');
    return true;
  }finally{
    PPA_CLOUD.loading=false;
  }
}


// === V263 TEST CLEANUP / PICKAXE PPA ======================================
// Remove ONLY the temporary V260 test items that were tagged v260Test.
// Legitimate player gear/pets are not touched.
function v263RemoveV260TestItems(){
  let changed=false;

  if(INV.equipped&&typeof INV.equipped==='object'){
    Object.keys(INV.equipped).forEach(function(slot){
      const it=INV.equipped[slot];
      if(it&&it.v260Test===true){
        INV.equipped[slot]=null;
        changed=true;
      }
    });
  }

  if(Array.isArray(INV.bag)){
    const before=INV.bag.length;
    INV.bag=INV.bag.filter(function(it){return !(it&&it.v260Test===true)});
    if(INV.bag.length!==before)changed=true;
  }

  if(INV.storage&&typeof INV.storage==='object'){
    ['personal','clan','premium'].forEach(function(key){
      if(!Array.isArray(INV.storage[key]))return;
      const before=INV.storage[key].length;
      INV.storage[key]=INV.storage[key].filter(function(it){return !(it&&it.v260Test===true)});
      if(INV.storage[key].length!==before)changed=true;
    });
  }

  if(changed){
    recomputeStats();
    P.hp=Math.min(P.hp,P.mhp);
    P.mp=Math.min(P.mp,P.mmp);
    saveGame();
    try{
      sendInvState();
      sendBlacksmithState();
      sendStorageState();
      sendPremiumState();
    }catch(_){}
    updateUI();
    showPickup('Временный комплект удалён','#c9b28a');
  }
  return changed;
}

// Keep the earlier requested one-time PPA help for the 200-PPA pickaxe,
// but do not repeatedly top up PPA on every launch.
function v263EnsurePickaxePpa(){
  if(INV.fartPickaxe)return;
  if(INV.v263PickaxePpaGranted)return;
  INV.ppa=Math.max(200,Math.floor(Number(INV.ppa)||0));
  INV.v263PickaxePpaGranted=true;
  saveGame();
  try{sendInvState();sendBlacksmithState();updateUI()}catch(_){}
}

// Exact access rule requested: level 21 through level 40 inclusive.
function fartZoneLevelAllowed(){
  const lvl=Math.floor(Number(P.lvl)||1);
  return lvl>=21&&lvl<=40;
}

// === RELEASE CHARACTER LOAD ================================================
function applyClass(cls){
  if(!cls)return;

  const _classKey=classKeyFromName(cls.name)||'assassin';
  P.cls=CLASS_BASE[_classKey].name;
  P.anim='idle';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;P.attacking=false;
  P.dir8=4;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0;
  PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0;
  cancelSmartAttack();

  // Persistent character only: restore the saved level/progression FIRST,
  // then calculate the selected class base at that level.
  if(P._saved&&P._saved.lvl)P.lvl=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(P._saved.lvl)||1));
  applyClassBaseProfile(_classKey,P.lvl);

  if(P._saved){var sv=P._saved;
    if(sv.lvl)P.lvl=sv.lvl;
    if(sv.xp!=null)P.xp=sv.xp;
    if(sv.kil!=null)P.kil=sv.kil;
    P.xn=xpRequiredForLevel(P.lvl);
    if(typeof sv.statPts==='number')P.statPts=sv.statPts;
    else if(typeof sv.talentPts==='number')P.statPts=sv.talentPts;
    else P.statPts=10+Math.max(0,(P.lvl||1)-1)*3;
    P.statAlloc=Object.assign(
      {hp:0,mp:0,atk:0,def:0,spd:0,atkspd:0,critdmg:0,crit:0,dodge:0},
      sv.statAlloc||{}
    );
    P.rebirths=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(sv.rebirths)||0));
    repairStatPointProgress();
  }

  INV.books.forEach(function(b){
    if(b.applied){
      if(b.id==='fire')P.dmgMul=1.5;
      if(b.id==='ice')P.slowMul=0.7;
      if(b.id==='lightning')P.chain=2;
    }
  });

  P.sp=P.baseSp;
  recomputeStats();
  P.hp=P.mhp;
  P.mp=P.mmp;
  saveGame();
  updateUI();
}

function beginGame(){
  document.getElementById('classSelectFrame').style.display='none';
  v263RemoveV260TestItems();
  v263EnsurePickaxePpa();
  if(__gameLoopStarted)return;
  __gameLoopStarted=true;
  const sc=SCENES[P.scene];
  const ln=document.getElementById('locName');
  ln.innerHTML=sc.name+'<div class=\"sub\">'+sc.sub+'</div>';
  ln.classList.add('show');
  setTimeout(()=>ln.classList.remove('show'),2500);
  PPAOnlineInit();
  let __v178LastLoopErrorAt=0;
  function loop(){
    try{
      update();
    }catch(err){
      const now=Date.now();
      if(now-__v178LastLoopErrorAt>1000){
        __v178LastLoopErrorAt=now;
        console.error('V178 update recovered',err);
      }
      // World-boss rewards must remain playable even if a secondary system
      // throws after the kill. The next frame continues normally.
      try{
        if(P.scene==='worldboss'&&WORLD_BOSS_LOOT_EVENT.active){
          transitioning=false;
          if(P.hp>0)P.dead=false;
          worldBossLootUpdate();
        }
      }catch(_){ }
    }
    try{
      render();
      try{if(window.__PPA_PERF_FRAME)window.__PPA_PERF_FRAME(performance.now())}catch(_){ }
    }catch(err){
      const now=Date.now();
      if(now-__v178LastLoopErrorAt>1000){
        __v178LastLoopErrorAt=now;
        console.error('V178 render recovered',err);
      }
    }
    requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }
  loop();
}

        var first=await PPA.ppaSaveGame(initialState,cloud.version);
        if(first&&Number.isFinite(Number(first.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(first.version);
        else if(first&&first.save&&Number.isFinite(Number(first.save.version)))PPA_CLOUD.version=Number(first.save.version);
        cloud={version:PPA_CLOUD.version,state:initialState};
        cloudHasCharacter=true;
      }else{
        console.info('PPA: new Telegram account detected; clearing foreign local character cache without reload.');
        ppaClearLocalCharacterCacheForAccountSwitch();
        ppaResetFreshCharacterInMemory(profileNick);
        savedNick=profileNick;
        localCachePresent=false;
      }

    savedNick=String(

       (P&&P._saved&&P._saved.playerName)||
       window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||
       localStorage.getItem('ppaPlayerNameV205')||'')
    ).trim();
    var savedNickOk=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(savedNick);
    var profileNickOk=/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]{3,18}$/u.test(profileNick);
    var nick=savedNickOk?savedNick:(profileNickOk?profileNick:'');
    if(nick){
      INV.playerName=nick;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=nick;
      try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',nick)}catch(_){}
    }
    // Existing V255 save wins during the one-time Gateway migration.
    // The server reads playerName from its own cloud save, so the client cannot
    // use this path as a free arbitrary rename.
    if(savedNickOk&&savedNick!==profileNick&&typeof PPA.ppaSyncNicknameFromSave==='function'){
      try{
        var syncNick=await PPA.ppaSyncNicknameFromSave();
        if(syncNick&&syncNick.ok&&syncNick.profile)PPA_CLOUD.profile=syncNick.profile;
        else if(syncNick&&syncNick.message)console.warn('PPA nickname migration:',syncNick.message);
      }catch(syncErr){console.warn('PPA nickname migration:',syncErr)}
    }

    var ck=(P._saved&&P._saved.cls)?classKeyFromName(P._saved.cls):'';
    if(!ck)ck=ppaProfileClassKey(profile);
    if(ck&&CLASS_BASE[ck]){
      applyClass({name:CLASS_BASE[ck].name});
      beginGame();
      return true;
    }
    return false;
  }catch(err){
    console.error('PPA Gateway bootstrap:',err);
    ppaShowGatewayError('Не удалось подтвердить Telegram-сессию. Закройте Mini App и откройте игру снова через бота.');
    return true;
  }finally{
    PPA_CLOUD.loading=false;
  }
}


// === V263 TEST CLEANUP / PICKAXE PPA ======================================
// Remove ONLY the temporary V260 test items that were tagged v260Test.
// Legitimate player gear/pets are not touched.
function v263RemoveV260TestItems(){
  let changed=false;

  if(INV.equipped&&typeof INV.equipped==='object'){
    Object.keys(INV.equipped).forEach(function(slot){
      const it=INV.equipped[slot];
      if(it&&it.v260Test===true){
        INV.equipped[slot]=null;
        changed=true;
      }
    });
  }

  if(Array.isArray(INV.bag)){
    const before=INV.bag.length;
    INV.bag=INV.bag.filter(function(it){return !(it&&it.v260Test===true)});
    if(INV.bag.length!==before)changed=true;
  }

  if(INV.storage&&typeof INV.storage==='object'){
    ['personal','clan','premium'].forEach(function(key){
      if(!Array.isArray(INV.storage[key]))return;
      const before=INV.storage[key].length;
      INV.storage[key]=INV.storage[key].filter(function(it){return !(it&&it.v260Test===true)});
      if(INV.storage[key].length!==before)changed=true;
    });
  }

  if(changed){
    recomputeStats();
    P.hp=Math.min(P.hp,P.mhp);
    P.mp=Math.min(P.mp,P.mmp);
    saveGame();
    try{
      sendInvState();
      sendBlacksmithState();
      sendStorageState();
      sendPremiumState();
    }catch(_){}
    updateUI();
    showPickup('Временный комплект удалён','#c9b28a');
  }
  return changed;
}

// Keep the earlier requested one-time PPA help for the 200-PPA pickaxe,
// but do not repeatedly top up PPA on every launch.
function v263EnsurePickaxePpa(){
  if(INV.fartPickaxe)return;
  if(INV.v263PickaxePpaGranted)return;
  INV.ppa=Math.max(200,Math.floor(Number(INV.ppa)||0));
  INV.v263PickaxePpaGranted=true;
  saveGame();
  try{sendInvState();sendBlacksmithState();updateUI()}catch(_){}
}

// Exact access rule requested: level 21 through level 40 inclusive.
function fartZoneLevelAllowed(){
  const lvl=Math.floor(Number(P.lvl)||1);
  return lvl>=21&&lvl<=40;
}

// === RELEASE CHARACTER LOAD ================================================
function applyClass(cls){
  if(!cls)return;

  const _classKey=classKeyFromName(cls.name)||'assassin';
  P.cls=CLASS_BASE[_classKey].name;
  P.anim='idle';P.animFrame=0;P.animTimer=0;P.attacking=false;
  P.dir8=4;
  PLAYER_CANNONBALLS.length=0;
  PLAYER_ARROWS.length=0;
  cancelSmartAttack();

  // Persistent character only: restore the saved level/progression FIRST,
  // then calculate the selected class base at that level.
  if(P._saved&&P._saved.lvl)P.lvl=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(P._saved.lvl)||1));
  applyClassBaseProfile(_classKey,P.lvl);

  if(P._saved){var sv=P._saved;
    if(sv.lvl)P.lvl=sv.lvl;
    if(sv.xp!=null)P.xp=sv.xp;
    if(sv.kil!=null)P.kil=sv.kil;
    P.xn=xpRequiredForLevel(P.lvl);
    if(typeof sv.statPts==='number')P.statPts=sv.statPts;
    else if(typeof sv.talentPts==='number')P.statPts=sv.talentPts;
    else P.statPts=10+Math.max(0,(P.lvl||1)-1)*3;
    P.statAlloc=Object.assign(
      {hp:0,mp:0,atk:0,def:0,spd:0,atkspd:0,critdmg:0,crit:0,dodge:0},
      sv.statAlloc||{}
    );
    P.rebirths=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(sv.rebirths)||0));
    repairStatPointProgress();
  }

  INV.books.forEach(function(b){
    if(b.applied){
      if(b.id==='fire')P.dmgMul=1.5;
      if(b.id==='ice')P.slowMul=0.7;
      if(b.id==='lightning')P.chain=2;
    }
  });

  P.sp=P.baseSp;
  recomputeStats();
  P.hp=P.mhp;
  P.mp=P.mmp;
  saveGame();
  updateUI();
}

// === V207 SEPARATE GRAM WALLET · TON ====================================
const GRAM_WALLET_WITHDRAW_FEE=0.10;
let GRAM_WALLET_OPEN=false;
const gramWalletSideTab=document.getElementById('gramWalletSideTab');
const gramWalletBackdrop=document.getElementById('gramWalletBackdrop');
const gramWalletPanel=document.getElementById('gramWalletPanel');
const gramWalletClose=document.getElementById('gramWalletClose');
const gramWalletFrame=document.getElementById('gramWalletFrame');

function gramWalletTelegramUser(){
  try{
    var u=window.Telegram&&Telegram.WebApp&&Telegram.WebApp.initDataUnsafe&&Telegram.WebApp.initDataUnsafe.user;
    if(u&&u.id){
      var n=[u.first_name,u.last_name].filter(Boolean).join(' ').trim()||u.username||('ID '+u.id);
      return {id:String(u.id),name:n,username:u.username||''};
    }
  }catch(_){}
  return {id:'',name:'Вне Telegram',username:''};
}
function gramWalletRoot(){
  if(!INV.gramWallet||typeof INV.gramWallet!=='object')INV.gramWallet={profiles:{}};
  if(!INV.gramWallet.profiles||typeof INV.gramWallet.profiles!=='object')INV.gramWallet.profiles={};
  return INV.gramWallet;
}
function gramWalletProfile(){
  var root=gramWalletRoot(),tg=gramWalletTelegramUser(),key=String(tg.id||'offline');
  var p=root.profiles[key];
  if(!p||typeof p!=='object')p=root.profiles[key]={connected:false,address:'',history:[],walletGram:null,updatedAt:0};
  p.connected=!!p.connected;p.address=String(p.address||'');if(!Array.isArray(p.history))p.history=[];
  if(!Number.isFinite(Number(p.walletGram)))p.walletGram=null;else p.walletGram=Math.max(0,Number(p.walletGram));
  return p;
}
function gramWalletHistory(text,amountText){

function gramWalletValidTonAddress(v){

  return /^(?:EQ|UQ|kQ|0Q)[A-Za-z0-9_-]{46}$/.test(v)||/^(?:-1|0):[0-9a-fA-F]{64}$/.test(v);
}

}

function renderChat(){
  document.querySelectorAll('.ppaChatTab').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('on',b.dataset.chatChannel===chat.channel));
  targetRow.classList.toggle('show',chat.channel==='private');
  target.textContent=chat.target||'Имя игрока';
  target.style.color=chat.target?'#dcb6a9':'#655651';
  if(chat.draft){draft.textContent=chat.draft;draft.classList.remove('placeholder')}else{draft.textContent='Нажми, чтобы написать…';draft.classList.add('placeholder')}
  let arr=(chat.messages[chat.channel]||[]);
  if(!arr.length)log.innerHTML='<div class="ppaChatEmpty">'+(chat.channel==='general'?'Общий чат':chat.channel==='clan'?'Чат клана':chat.channel==='party'?'Беседа группы':'Личные сообщения')+'<br>Сообщений пока нет</div>';
  else log.innerHTML=arr.slice(-80).map(m=>{
    if(m.system)return '<div class="ppaMsg system"><span class="time">'+fmtTime(m.ts)+'</span>'+esc(m.text)+'</div>';
    let cls='ppaMsg'+(m.self?' self':'')+(chat.channel==='private'?' private':'');
    let to=(chat.channel==='private'&&m.target)?' → '+esc(m.target):'';
    return '<div class="'+cls+'"><span class="time">'+fmtTime(m.ts)+'</span><span class="from">'+esc(m.from||'Игрок')+to+':</span> '+esc(m.text||'')+'</div>';
  }).join('');
  log.scrollTop=log.scrollHeight;
  chat.unread[chat.channel]=0;renderMini();chatSave();
}

function smartKeyboardTop(){
  let sug=chat.edit==='draft'?chatSuggestions():[],h='';
  if(chat.edit==='draft'){
    h+='<div class="ppaKbSmart"><span class="ppaKbSmartLabel">СЛОВА</span>';
    (sug.length?sug:['…']).slice(0,4).forEach(w=>{if(w!=='…')h+=smartChip(w,'suggest',w,'ppaKbSuggest')});
    h+='</div>';
    h+='<div class="ppaKbSmart"><span class="ppaKbSmartLabel">😊</span>'+CHAT_EMOJI.map(e=>smartChip(e,'emoji',e,'ppaKbEmoji')).join('')+'</div>';
    let hist=(chat.history||[]).slice(-3).reverse();
    if(hist.length){h+='<div class="ppaKbSmart"><span class="ppaKbSmartLabel">↶</span>'+hist.map(t=>smartChip(t.length>28?t.slice(0,27)+'…':t,'history',t,'ppaKbHistory')).join('')+'</div>'}
  }
  return h;
}
function kbBtn(label,act,extra){return '<button class="ppaKb '+(extra||'')+'" type="button" data-kact="'+esc(act||'key')+'" data-k="'+esc(label)+'">'+esc(label)+'</button>'}
function renderKeyboard(){
  let rows=KB[chat.layout]||KB.ru,h=smartKeyboardTop();
  rows.forEach((r,idx)=>{h+='<div class="ppaKbRow">';if(idx===rows.length-1)h+=kbBtn('⇧','shift','act'+(chat.shift?' shiftOn':''));r.forEach(k=>h+=kbBtn(chat.shift?k.toUpperCase():k,'key'));if(idx===rows.length-1)h+=kbBtn('⌫','back','act');h+='</div>'});
  h+='<div class="ppaKbRow">'+kbBtn('📱 ANDROID','native','act ppaKbMode')+kbBtn(chat.layout==='ru'?'EN':'RU','lang','act')+kbBtn('ПРОБЕЛ','space','act space')+kbBtn(',','key','act')+kbBtn('.','key','act')+kbBtn('?','key','act')+kbBtn('!','key','act')+kbBtn('ГОТОВО','done','done')+'</div>';
  kb.innerHTML=h;
}
function editValue(){return chat.edit==='target'?chat.target:chat.draft}
function setEditValue(v){
  if(chat.edit==='target')chat.target=String(v||'').slice(0,24);
  else chat.draft=String(v||'').slice(0,180);
  renderChat();if(kb.classList.contains('open')){root.classList.add('typing');applyKbFocus()}if(document.activeElement===nativeInput&&nativeInput.value!==editValue())nativeInput.value=editValue()
}
function kbAction(act,key){
  let v=editValue();
  if(act==='key'){
    let out=key;
    if(chat.edit==='draft'&&chatNeedsCapital(v)&&/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё]$/.test(out))out=out.toUpperCase();
    if(v.length<(chat.edit==='target'?24:180))setEditValue(v+out);
    if(chat.shift){chat.shift=false;renderKeyboard()}
  }
  else if(act==='space')setEditValue(v+' ');
  else if(act==='back')setEditValue(v.slice(0,-1));
  else if(act==='shift'){chat.shift=!chat.shift;renderKeyboard()}
  else if(act==='lang'){chat.layout=chat.layout==='ru'?'en':'ru';chat.shift=false;renderKeyboard();chatSave()}
  else if(act==='suggest'){chatReplaceWord(key)}
  else if(act==='emoji'){chat.edit='draft';let d=String(chat.draft||'');setEditValue(d+(d&&!/\s$/.test(d)?' ':'')+key+' ');renderKeyboard()}
  else if(act==='history'){chat.edit='draft';setEditValue(String(key||''));renderKeyboard()}
  else if(act==='native'){let w=chat.edit;openNativeKeyboard(w)}
  else if(act==='done')closeKeyboard();
}
function sendChat(){
  let t=String(chat.draft||'').trim();if(!t)return;
  if(chat.channel==='private'&&!String(chat.target||'').trim()){chat.edit='target';openKeyboard('target');return}
  let _from=(typeof INV!=='undefined'&&String(INV.playerName||'').trim())||String(window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'').trim()||'Вы';
  let m={ts:Date.now(),from:_from,text:t,target:chat.channel==='private'?chat.target:'',self:true};
  chat.history=Array.isArray(chat.history)?chat.history:[];
  if(!chat.history.length||chat.history[chat.history.length-1]!==t)chat.history.push(t);
  if(chat.history.length>12)chat.history=chat.history.slice(-12);
  (chat.messages[chat.channel]||(chat.messages[chat.channel]=[])).push(m);
  if(chat.messages[chat.channel].length>80)chat.messages[chat.channel]=chat.messages[chat.channel].slice(-80);
  chat.draft='';chatSave();renderChat();
  try{window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('ppa-chat-send',{detail:{channel:chat.channel,target:m.target,text:m.text,ts:m.ts}}))}catch(_e){}
}
window.PPA_CHAT_RECEIVE=function(channel,from,textMessage,opts){
  opts=opts||{};channel=CH[channel]?channel:'general';
  let m={ts:Date.now(),from:from||'Игрок',text:String(textMessage||''),target:opts.target||'',self:false,system:!!opts.system};
  (chat.messages[channel]||(chat.messages[channel]=[])).push(m);
  if(chat.messages[channel].length>80)chat.messages[channel]=chat.messages[channel].slice(-80);
  if(!(root.classList.contains('open')&&chat.channel===channel))chat.unread[channel]=(Number(chat.unread[channel])||0)+1;
  chatSave();if(root.classList.contains('open')&&chat.channel===channel)renderChat();else renderMini();
};
mini.addEventListener('click',openChat);
document.getElementById('ppaChatMinimize').addEventListener('click',closeChat);
document.querySelectorAll('.ppaChatTab').forEach(b=>b.addEventListener('click',()=>setChannel(b.dataset.chatChannel)));
draft.addEventListener('click',()=>openKeyboard('draft'));
target.addEventListener('click',()=>openKeyboard('target'));
document.getElementById('ppaChatSend').addEventListener('click',sendChat);
kb.addEventListener('pointerdown',function(ev){let b=ev.target.closest('[data-kact]');if(!b||!kb.contains(b))return;ev.preventDefault();ev.stopPropagation();kbAction(b.dataset.kact,b.dataset.k||'')});
nativeInput.addEventListener('input',function(){
  let v=String(nativeInput.value||'').slice(0,chat.edit==='target'?24:180);
  if(chat.edit==='target')chat.target=v;else chat.draft=v;
  if(chat.edit==='target'){target.textContent=chat.target||'Имя игрока';target.style.color=chat.target?'#dcb6a9':'#655651'}
  else{if(chat.draft){draft.textContent=chat.draft;draft.classList.remove('placeholder')}else{draft.textContent='Нажми, чтобы написать…';draft.classList.add('placeholder')}}
});
nativeInput.addEventListener('keydown',function(ev){
  if(ev.key!=='Enter')return;ev.preventDefault();
  if(chat.edit==='target'){chatSave();closeKeyboard();renderChat()}else{sendChat();nativeInput.value='';try{nativeInput.focus({preventScroll:true})}catch(_e){}}
});
nativeInput.addEventListener('blur',function(){setTimeout(function(){if(document.activeElement===nativeInput)return;nativeBar.classList.remove('on');nativeBar.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');root.classList.remove('nativeTyping');ppaChatResetAndroidPosition();draft.classList.remove('kbfocus');target.classList.remove('kbfocus');chatSave()},160)});
document.getElementById('ppaChatBackToGameKb').style.display='none';
document.getElementById('ppaChatNativeClose').style.display='none';
renderMini();renderChat();
})();
</script>
