<script>
(function(){
  var back=document.getElementById('ppaRenameBackdrop'),
      panel=document.getElementById('ppaRenamePanel'),
      input=document.getElementById('ppaRenameInput'),
      hint=document.getElementById('ppaRenameHint'),
      cur=document.getElementById('ppaRenameCurrentName');

  function currentName(){
    try{return String((typeof INV!=='undefined'&&INV.playerName)||window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME||'Игрок').trim()||'Игрок'}catch(_){return 'Игрок'}
  }
  function validate(v){
    v=String(v||'').trim();
    if(v.length<3||v.length>18)return 'Ник должен содержать 3–18 символов';
    if(!/^[A-Za-zА-Яа-яЁё0-9_]+$/u.test(v))return 'Разрешены только буквы, цифры и _';
    if(v.toLocaleLowerCase()===currentName().toLocaleLowerCase())return 'Это уже ваш текущий ник';
    return '';
  }
  function positionForKeyboard(){
    if(!window.visualViewport)return;
    try{
      back.style.height=Math.max(120,window.visualViewport.height)+'px';
      back.style.transform='translateY('+Math.max(0,window.visualViewport.offsetTop)+'px)';
    }catch(_){}
  }
  window.openRenameModal=function(){
    if(typeof INV!=='undefined'&&(INV.renameCards||0)<=0)return;
    try{if(typeof closeInventory==='function')closeInventory()}catch(_){}
    cur.textContent=currentName();
    input.value='';
    hint.textContent='Кириллица, латиница, цифры и _';
    hint.classList.remove('bad');
    back.classList.add('on');back.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
    positionForKeyboard();
    setTimeout(function(){try{input.focus()}catch(_){}},80);
  };
  window.closeRenameModal=function(){
    back.classList.remove('on');back.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
    try{input.blur()}catch(_){}
    back.style.height='';back.style.transform='';
  };
  input.addEventListener('input',function(){
    var e=validate(input.value);
    hint.textContent=e||'Ник подходит ✓';
    hint.classList.toggle('bad',!!e);
  });
  document.getElementById('ppaRenameCancel').addEventListener('click',window.closeRenameModal);
  document.getElementById('ppaRenameConfirm').addEventListener('click',async function(){
    var btn=this;
    var n=String(input.value||'').trim(),e=validate(n);
    if(e){hint.textContent=e;hint.classList.add('bad');return}
    if(typeof INV==='undefined'||(INV.renameCards||0)<=0){
      hint.textContent='Карточка уже использована';hint.classList.add('bad');return;
    }

    var serverResult=null;
    var gatewayRename=false;
    try{
      gatewayRename=(typeof PPA!=='undefined'&&typeof PPA.ppaRequestNicknameChange==='function'&&
        (typeof PPA.isAvailable!=='function'||PPA.isAvailable()));
    }catch(_){gatewayRename=false}

    if(gatewayRename){
      btn.disabled=true;
      hint.classList.remove('bad');
      hint.textContent='Проверяем ник на сервере…';
      try{
        serverResult=await PPA.ppaRequestNicknameChange(n,'rename_card_v255_'+Date.now());
      }catch(err){
        btn.disabled=false;
        hint.textContent='Нет связи с сервером. Карточка не потрачена.';
        hint.classList.add('bad');
        return;
      }
      if(!serverResult||serverResult.ok!==true){
        btn.disabled=false;
        hint.textContent=(serverResult&&serverResult.message)?String(serverResult.message):'Сервер отклонил смену ника. Карточка не потрачена.';
        hint.classList.add('bad');
        return;
      }
      if(serverResult.profile&&serverResult.profile.nickname)n=String(serverResult.profile.nickname).trim()||n;
      try{if(typeof PPA_CLOUD!=='undefined'&&serverResult.profile)PPA_CLOUD.profile=serverResult.profile}catch(_){}
    }

    // Списываем карточку только после успешной серверной проверки/смены.
    INV.renameCards=Math.max(0,(Number(INV.renameCards)||0)-1);
    INV.playerName=n;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=n;
    try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',n)}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof PPA_ONLINE!=='undefined')PPA_ONLINE.selfName=n}catch(_){}
    try{saveGame()}catch(_){}
    try{sendInvState()}catch(_){}
    try{sendPremiumState()}catch(_){}
    try{showPickup('✒ Имя изменено: '+n,'#f0c56f')}catch(_){}
    btn.disabled=false;
    window.closeRenameModal();
  });
  back.addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.target===back)window.closeRenameModal()});
  input.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){
    if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();document.getElementById('ppaRenameConfirm').click()}
    else if(e.key==='Escape'){window.closeRenameModal()}
  });
  if(window.visualViewport){
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',positionForKeyboard);
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll',positionForKeyboard);
  }

  // Premium shop bridge. Server shop can call this when a rename card is purchased.
  window.PPA_GRANT_RENAME_CARD=function(n){
    n=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(n)||1));
    if(typeof INV==='undefined')return 0;
    INV.renameCards=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(INV.renameCards)||0))+n;
    try{saveGame();sendInvState();sendPremiumState()}catch(_){}
    return INV.renameCards;
  };
})();
</script>

<!-- V209: native storage scrolling + Black Market phone scroll/footer fix + hide ONLINE badge in phone menus. -->
<script id="v209PhoneMenuChatGuard">
(function(){
  function isPhone(){try{return window.matchMedia('(max-width:700px)').matches}catch(_){return false}}
  function visibleByStyle(el){if(!el)return false;try{return getComputedStyle(el).display!=='none'&&getComputedStyle(el).visibility!=='hidden'}catch(_){return false}}
  function isBlockingOpen(){
    var checks=[
      ['premiumPanel','open'],['eventsPanel','open'],['gramWalletPanel','open'],
      ['dungeonGateMenu','on'],['dungeonTeleportMenu','on'],['inv','on'],['ppaItemInspectShade','on']
    ];
    for(var i=0;i<checks.length;i++){var e=document.getElementById(checks[i][0]);if(e&&e.classList.contains(checks[i][1]))return true}
    var frames=['charFrame','blacksmithFrame','storageFrame','merchantFrame','auctionFrame','blackmarketFrame','clanFrame','arenaMenuFrame'];
    for(var j=0;j<frames.length;j++){var f=document.getElementById(frames[j]);if(f&&visibleByStyle(f))return true}
    return false;
  }
  function sync(){
    var blocked=!!isBlockingOpen();
    var chat=document.getElementById('ppaChatRoot');
    if(chat)chat.classList.toggle('phoneMenuHidden',blocked);
    // V209: ONLINE badge must not cover phone modal/shop headers.
    var badge=document.getElementById('ppaOnlineBadge');
    if(badge)badge.style.display=(isPhone()&&blocked)?'none':'';
  }

  };
  input.addEventListener('input',function(){
    var e=validate(input.value);
    hint.textContent=e||'Ник подходит ✓';
    hint.classList.toggle('bad',!!e);
  });
  document.getElementById('ppaRenameCancel').addEventListener('click',window.closeRenameModal);
  document.getElementById('ppaRenameConfirm').addEventListener('click',async function(){
    var btn=this;
    var n=String(input.value||'').trim(),e=validate(n);
    if(e){hint.textContent=e;hint.classList.add('bad');return}
    if(typeof INV==='undefined'||(INV.renameCards||0)<=0){
      hint.textContent='Карточка уже использована';hint.classList.add('bad');return;
    }

    var serverResult=null;
    var gatewayRename=false;
    try{
      gatewayRename=(typeof PPA!=='undefined'&&typeof PPA.ppaRequestNicknameChange==='function'&&
        (typeof PPA.isAvailable!=='function'||PPA.isAvailable()));
    }catch(_){gatewayRename=false}

    if(gatewayRename){
      btn.disabled=true;
      hint.classList.remove('bad');
      hint.textContent='Проверяем ник на сервере…';
      try{
        serverResult=await PPA.ppaRequestNicknameChange(n,'rename_card_v255_'+Date.now());
      }catch(err){
        btn.disabled=false;
        hint.textContent='Нет связи с сервером. Карточка не потрачена.';
        hint.classList.add('bad');
        return;
      }
      if(!serverResult||serverResult.ok!==true){
        btn.disabled=false;
        hint.textContent=(serverResult&&serverResult.message)?String(serverResult.message):'Сервер отклонил смену ника. Карточка не потрачена.';
        hint.classList.add('bad');
        return;
      }
      if(serverResult.profile&&serverResult.profile.nickname)n=String(serverResult.profile.nickname).trim()||n;
      try{if(typeof PPA_CLOUD!=='undefined'&&serverResult.profile)PPA_CLOUD.profile=serverResult.profile}catch(_){}
    }

    // Списываем карточку только после успешной серверной проверки/смены.
    INV.renameCards=Math.max(0,(Number(INV.renameCards)||0)-1);
    INV.playerName=n;window.PPA_PLAYER_NAME=n;
    try{localStorage.setItem('ppaPlayerNameV205',n)}catch(_){}
    try{if(typeof PPA_ONLINE!=='undefined')PPA_ONLINE.selfName=n}catch(_){}
    try{saveGame()}catch(_){}
    try{sendInvState()}catch(_){}
    try{sendPremiumState()}catch(_){}
    try{showPickup('✒ Имя изменено: '+n,'#f0c56f')}catch(_){}
    btn.disabled=false;
    window.closeRenameModal();
  });
  back.addEventListener('click',function(e){if(e.target===back)window.closeRenameModal()});
  input.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){
    if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();document.getElementById('ppaRenameConfirm').click()}
    else if(e.key==='Escape'){window.closeRenameModal()}
  });
  if(window.visualViewport){
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('resize',positionForKeyboard);
    window.visualViewport.addEventListener('scroll',positionForKeyboard);
  }

  // Premium shop bridge. Server shop can call this when a rename card is purchased.
  window.PPA_GRANT_RENAME_CARD=function(n){
    n=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Number(n)||1));
    if(typeof INV==='undefined')return 0;
    INV.renameCards=Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(INV.renameCards)||0))+n;
    try{saveGame();sendInvState();sendPremiumState()}catch(_){}
    return INV.renameCards;
  };
})();
</script>

<!-- V209: native storage scrolling + Black Market phone scroll/footer fix + hide ONLINE badge in phone menus. -->
<script id="v209PhoneMenuChatGuard">
(function(){
  function isPhone(){try{return window.matchMedia('(max-width:700px)').matches}catch(_){return false}}
  function visibleByStyle(el){if(!el)return false;try{return getComputedStyle(el).display!=='none'&&getComputedStyle(el).visibility!=='hidden'}catch(_){return false}}
  function isBlockingOpen(){
    var checks=[
      ['premiumPanel','open'],['eventsPanel','open'],['gramWalletPanel','open'],
      ['dungeonGateMenu','on'],['dungeonTeleportMenu','on'],['inv','on'],['ppaItemInspectShade','on']
    ];
    for(var i=0;i<checks.length;i++){var e=document.getElementById(checks[i][0]);if(e&&e.classList.contains(checks[i][1]))return true}
    var frames=['charFrame','blacksmithFrame','storageFrame','merchantFrame','auctionFrame','blackmarketFrame','clanFrame','arenaMenuFrame'];
    for(var j=0;j<frames.length;j++){var f=document.getElementById(frames[j]);if(f&&visibleByStyle(f))return true}
    return false;
  }
  function sync(){
    var blocked=!!isBlockingOpen();
    var chat=document.getElementById('ppaChatRoot');
    if(chat)chat.classList.toggle('phoneMenuHidden',blocked);
    // V209: ONLINE badge must not cover phone modal/shop headers.
    var badge=document.getElementById('ppaOnlineBadge');
    if(badge)badge.style.display=(isPhone()&&blocked)?'none':'';
  }
  function start(){
    var ids=['premiumPanel','eventsPanel','gramWalletPanel','dungeonGateMenu','dungeonTeleportMenu','inv','ppaItemInspectShade','charFrame','blacksmithFrame','storageFrame','merchantFrame','auctionFrame','blackmarketFrame','clanFrame','arenaMenuFrame'];
    var mo=new MutationObserver(sync);
    ids.forEach(function(id){var e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)mo.observe(e,{attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class','style','aria-hidden']})});
    window.addEventListener('resize',sync,{passive:true});
    window.addEventListener('orientationchange',function(){setTimeout(sync,120)},{passive:true});
    document.addEventListener('click',function(){setTimeout(sync,0)},true);
    window.PPA_SYNC_PHONE_CHAT_VISIBILITY=sync;
    sync();
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',start,{once:true});else start();
})();
</script>


<style id="v210GlobalMenuChatFix">
/* V210: chat must never sit on top of any full menu/house, on tablet or phone. */
#ppaChatRoot.phoneMenuHidden{display:none!important}
</style>
<script id="v210StorageFullInventoryFix">
(function(){
  // Full inventory view for the Keeper. V209 only sent INV.bag (wearable gear),
  // which is why Storage showed e.g. 12/100 while Character inventory had many more stacks.
  function n(v){return Math.max(0,Math.floor(Number(v)||0))}
  function art(src){return src||''}
  function add(out,o){if(o&&n(o.count)>0){o.count=n(o.count);out.push(o)}}
  function refKey(kind,id){return 'stack:'+kind+':'+String(id||'')}

  function baseStack(kind,id,name,count,rarity,img,icon,typeName,useText){
    return {uid:refKey(kind,id),kind:kind,refId:String(id||''),storageRef:kind+':'+String(id||''),name:name||'Предмет',count:n(count),rarity:rarity||'common',img:art(img),icon:icon||'◆',ic:icon||'◆',typeName:typeName||'',useText:useText||''};
  }
  function consumableCount(id){
    if(id==='hp_small')return INV.potions.hp||0;
    if(id==='hp_medium')return INV.potions.hpMedium||0;
    if(id==='hp_large')return INV.potions.hpLarge||0;
    if(id==='mp_small')return INV.potions.mp||0;
    if(id==='mp_medium')return INV.potions.mpMedium||0;
    if(id==='mp_large')return INV.potions.mpLarge||0;
    if(id==='magic_small')return INV.consumables.magicSmall||0;
    if(id==='atk_speed_small')return INV.consumables.atkSpeedSmall||0;
    if(id==='run_speed_small')return INV.consumables.speedSmall||0;
    if(id==='phys_small')return INV.consumables.physSmall||0;
    if(id==='xp_scroll')return INV.consumables.xpScroll||0;
    if(id==='premium_hp_regen')return INV.consumables.premiumHpRegen||0;
    if(id==='premium_mp_regen')return INV.consumables.premiumMpRegen||0;
    if(id==='luck_coin')return INV.consumables.luckCoin||0;
    if(id==='portal_stone')return INV.consumables.portalStone||0;
    if(id==='rename_card')return INV.renameCards||0;
    return 0;
  }
  function setConsumableCount(id,val){
    val=n(val);
    if(id==='hp_small')INV.potions.hp=val;
    else if(id==='hp_medium')INV.potions.hpMedium=val;
    else if(id==='hp_large')INV.potions.hpLarge=val;
    else if(id==='mp_small')INV.potions.mp=val;
    else if(id==='mp_medium')INV.potions.mpMedium=val;
    else if(id==='mp_large')INV.potions.mpLarge=val;
    else if(id==='magic_small')INV.consumables.magicSmall=val;
    else if(id==='atk_speed_small')INV.consumables.atkSpeedSmall=val;
    else if(id==='run_speed_small')INV.consumables.speedSmall=val;
    else if(id==='phys_small')INV.consumables.physSmall=val;
    else if(id==='xp_scroll')INV.consumables.xpScroll=val;
    else if(id==='premium_hp_regen')INV.consumables.premiumHpRegen=val;
    else if(id==='premium_mp_regen')INV.consumables.premiumMpRegen=val;
    else if(id==='luck_coin')INV.consumables.luckCoin=val;
    else if(id==
